# Paybacks (1.62.0, part 2 — W6–W10) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A deposit that pays you back for spending — a friend's Venmo for dinner — is filed against that spending from Telegram's `/categorize` (**It's a payback »**): the row is re-typed to a `CREDIT` marked `REIMBURSEMENT`, kept that way by a new `asReimbursement` sync override, filed under the spending category, and logged as `payback-filed` with Undo and Redo; a refused category never shows Wealthfolio's own text (W9); and "Make every <pattern> deposit a payback" makes the two rules that do it on import (W10).

**Architecture:** Pure shared modules decide; each host lends its reads and writes. `shared/payback.ts` holds `planPayback` (facts re-read right before a write → the writes, or a refusal in its own words), `filePayback` (override → re-type → category → log, saying plainly what stands when a step fails) and `makePaybackRules` (W10); `shared/payback-row.ts` is the row a payback reads (with its stored instant) and the one re-type write, shared with the change log's Undo planner; `shared/payback-copy.ts` is every sentence. The sync (`shared/sync-core.ts`, one engine for both hosts) plans an `asReimbursement` row as `CREDIT`/`REIMBURSEMENT` while its bank row is money in on a cash account. The change log learns `payback-filed` (`shared/change-entries.ts`, `shared/change-undo.ts`, `shared/change-undo-run.ts`). Telegram: `shared/categorize-menu.ts` draws the payback grid and the result screens; `companion/src/categorize.ts` runs the tap through `companion/src/payback-host.ts` (REST), wired in `companion/src/index.ts`.

**Tech Stack:** TypeScript, vitest (root project: jsdom, `src/` + `shared/`; companion project: node, `companion/src/`), Telegram Bot API (legacy `Markdown`), Wealthfolio REST (companion), Wealthfolio 3.8–3.10.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-line-up-and-paybacks-design.md` — rulings W6–W10 (approved by Nick, 2026-10-14). Lining up (W1–W5) and the platform work (U1–U4) are planned on their own branches; W11 (paybacks in the app) is a later small plan that consumes this part's fixed interface. Branch `feat/paybacks` in the worktree `wf-paybacks`, off main 71435fb (v1.61.0), spec head d344c96. Every Replace block below was checked against the files at d344c96.

**Verified facts this plan builds on (Q1):**
- **(a) Wealthfolio, read at v3.8.0, v3.9.0 and v3.10.0.**
  - `classify_activity` (crates/spending/src/activity_classification.rs:98-130 at 3.8.0/3.9.0, :100-132 at 3.10.0): on a CASH account `DEPOSIT | TRANSFER_IN | INTEREST` is Income (:108/:110), a `CREDIT` whose subtype is `REFUND`, `REBATE` or `REIMBURSEMENT` is ExpenseRefund (:116/:118) — `spending_amount` is `-amount` (:32) — and a bare `CREDIT` is Ignored (:121/:123). On a CREDIT_CARD **every** `CREDIT` is ExpenseRefund, whatever its subtype (:126/:128). So a payback is a cash `DEPOSIT` re-typed to `CREDIT`/`REIMBURSEMENT`, and a card needs none.
  - The bucket check: `assign_category` calls `ensure_activity_assignment_allowed(…, enforce_bucket = true)` (crates/spending/src/cash_activities/service.rs:769-780, check at :957-1018 at 3.10.0; :901-962 at 3.8.0): `classify_activity_for_aggregation` → `cash_flow_bucket_from_classification` → `taxonomy_for_bucket`; Neutral answers "Neutral transfers cannot be categorized. Change or unlink the transfer if it should count as spending.", a mismatch "{Income|Spending|Saving} activities can only use {income|spending|savings} categories. Categories label the cash-flow bucket; they do not change it." `ensure_activity_in_spending_scope` (:1063-1098 at 3.10.0; :1007-1042 at 3.8.0) answers "Spending tracking is disabled", "Activity account is not opted into spending tracking", "Activity account does not support spending tracking". `unassign_category` passes `enforce_bucket = false` (:782-786), so a payback's Undo can take the category off after the row is a deposit again.
  - The re-type over `/activities/bulk`: `ActivityUpdate.subtype` deserializes through `subtype_patch_format` — absent is `None` (kept), present is `Some`, and `null`/`""` read as `Some("")` (activities_model.rs:679-683, :1858-1868 at 3.10.0; :604, :1719 at 3.8.0/3.9.0); the bulk update stores `Some(v) if v.trim().is_empty() => None` (storage-sqlite activities/repository.rs:1367-1371 at 3.10.0; :1378-1379 at 3.8.0/3.9.0). So **an explicit `''` clears the subtype**; an omitted one is kept. `canonicalize_subtype` stores `REIMBURSEMENT` upper-case. A `CREDIT` needs no asset (`requires_symbol(CREDIT)` is false, activities_constants.rs:386 at 3.10.0); a `$CASH-USD` symbol is read as cash and the asset cleared (`cash_symbol_currency`, activities_service.rs:903-926 at 3.10.0). The cash effect is unchanged: `type_directed_cash_effect` books `+|amount|` for `CREDIT` exactly as for `DEPOSIT` (portfolio/economic_events.rs:349-364 at 3.10.0). `activity_date` accepts RFC 3339 (`validate_activity_date`, activities_model.rs:24-42 at 3.8.0), so a re-type can write the stored instant back unchanged.
  - An existing assignment when the type flips: assignments live in their own table (`activity_taxonomy_assignments`, storage-sqlite migration 2026-05-25-000001_spending_module) that an activity update never touches; the bucket is checked only on assign. So a payback is offered only for a row `/categorize` still lists as having no category (re-checked at the tap), and its Undo takes the one spending filing off.
  - Wealthfolio's rule run (`rerun_all`, categorization_rules/service.rs:162-247 at 3.10.0) assigns without a bucket check, and its matcher honours a rule's `activity_type` and its account (`match_compiled`, matcher.rs:80-91 at 3.8.0/3.9.0): W10's category rule is therefore scoped to the account **and** to `CREDIT`.
- **(b) The sync.** A feed row's type and subtype are decided once, in Phase A (`mapTransactionWithSource`, then the `notTransfer` override: shared/sync-core.ts:2127-2135 at d344c96), carried onto the `FeedTx` (:2519-2532), compared by `changed()` (shared/reconcile.ts:99-124: a different type is an update; a subtype is only ever added) and written by `toActivityCreate`/`toActivityUpdate` (sync-core.ts:3186-3237, the subtype key omitted when there is none). Without an override, the next sync updates a hand-made `CREDIT` back to `DEPOSIT` — the revert the spec names. Both hosts run this one `runSyncCore`, so one change keeps a payback on both. Both hosts' override readers keep a flag they do not know (`isEntry`, shared/sync-overrides.ts:117-130, P3-B), so a 1.61 reader ignores `asReimbursement` and reverts the row: hence W8.
- **(c) Telegram.** `/categorize`'s money-in row shows the income grid and "Spending categories »" (`useIncomeGrid`/`incomeEligible`, shared/categorize-menu.ts:619-657); a spending tap there was `assign`, which Wealthfolio refuses on a `DEPOSIT`, and the controller forwarded the refusal raw (`ASSIGN_FAILED_PREFIX`, companion/src/categorize.ts:360, :1184). The controller never computes a guess for an income-eligible row (`withGuess`, :803-808). `REFUSED_TEXT`/`assignabilityOf` (shared/cash-flow-bucket.ts:137-152) already give the add-on's own sentences for the bucket. Callback data is `cz:<generation>:<index>` (:421), at most 22 bytes even at `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`.
- **(d) Undo.** The companion's REST unassign is `DELETE /spending/activities/{id}/assignments/{taxonomyId}` (`unassignActivityCategory`, companion/src/wealthfolio.ts:393-402). The app shows "Undo this from Telegram: /changes" for every kind in `COMPANION_ONLY_KINDS` (shared/change-undo.ts:35-37, :100-104).

**Deviations from the spec and the rulings (decided, do not "fix"):**
- **Two refusals beyond the ruling's list**, each in its own words: a row the sync manages (a held transfer, a starting balance, a correction) and a row not from the bank feed (no tx id, or an account no feed maps to) — the override is keyed by tx id, and W11 shares the planner.
- **W10's transaction rule has two new limits**, `onlyMoneyIn` and `accountId` on `MappingRule`. A rule's type is otherwise direction-blind (`matchRule`, shared/mapper.ts), so a plain `CREDIT` rule for "VENMO CASHOUT" would book a payment OUT with those words as money in. `ruleApplies` honours them; a pre-1.62 reader ignores them, which is why the offer waits behind the same installed-add-on gate (W8). `sameMappingRule` (now the one rule identity, used by Undo) and `mappingRuleOf` carry them, so Undo removes exactly that rule and Redo puts it back with its limits.
- **W10's Wealthfolio rule is scoped to the account (as in project 3) and to `CREDIT`** — Wealthfolio's rule run assigns without a bucket check (a), so a rule for any type would file matching deposits (income) under a spending category. `category-rule-created` records `activityType`, and Redo re-creates it.
- **A re-type writes back the stored instant exactly** (`PaybackRow.instant`), never a bare date, so it can never move a row to the previous day west of UTC (the W1 problem), before or after W1/W2 land.
- **The entry names the row's key under every feed of its account** (`subject.txKeys`, normally one), as Remove for good does; the override goes on each.
- **The Undo planner reads the row through `UndoIo.paybacks.read`** (companion only — the kind is companion-only), and `PaybackRow`, `isPaybackRow` and `paybackRetype` live in a leaf, `shared/payback-row.ts`: the planner cannot import `shared/payback.ts` (payback → fix-row → change-undo-run → change-undo would be a cycle).
- **The Filed screen after a payback has no ↩ Undo of its own**; it says "Undo it from /changes." The one-tap guess is never offered on the payback grid.
- **W9 covers every `assignActivityCategory` failure** in `/categorize` and `/recategorize`: a first filing, a move's new category and a move's Undo. An unassign that fails keeps its present words.
- **"The refusal names the app's Change log"**: the app cannot undo a payback (companion-only), so the copy names it as where paybacks show once the add-on is updated. The same sentence refuses the filing, its Undo, its Redo and W10.
- **The app's gate:** a payback's entry shows "Update the companion first" (disabled Undo) while the companion is older than 1.62.0, else "Undo this from Telegram: /changes"; with no companion, nothing.
- **"Expiring" (Review Focus) is the override's year**: a transaction override is pruned a year after it was set, on the next write of the document; the sync then types the row as usual.
- **Copy the rulings did not spell out** is fixed in `shared/payback-copy.ts` (Task 2) and `shared/categorize-menu.ts` (Task 7), quoted in Global Constraints.

**Task order:** the override and the gate (1); the planner, the copy and the change kind (2); the filing runner (3); Undo and Redo with both gates (4); the sync keeps a payback (5); the menu (6); plain words for a refused category, W9 (7); the payback in Telegram end to end (8); "Make every … deposit a payback", W10 (9); docs, the forced-date run, every gate (10).

## Global Constraints

- **The fixed interface W11 consumes — these exact names:** the override `asReimbursement` in `TX_OVERRIDE_FLAGS` (shared/sync-overrides.ts); the change kind `payback-filed` — `subject` the row and its tx key(s), `before: { activityType: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: string | null, categoryId: null }`, `after: { activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', categoryId, categoryName, accountId }`; `PAYBACK_SINCE = '1.62.0'` (shared/version.ts) with `addonUnderstandsPaybacks` (companion side, on `installed_addon_version`) and `companionUnderstandsPaybacks` (app side); `planPayback(facts) → { ok: true, writes } | { ok: false, reason }` (shared/payback.ts).
- **Writes only through the existing paths.** Overrides: `editSyncOverrides`, which re-reads right before it writes and never overwrites an unreadable document. The row: one update through the bulk endpoint (`saveMany`). The category: `PUT /spending/activities/{id}/assignments`; Undo's `DELETE …/assignments/{taxonomy}`. `mapping_rules`: re-read right before it is written, never rewritten when unreadable. Wealthfolio rules: `createCategorizationRule` at priority 50 in the spending taxonomy. The change log: `recordCompanionChanges` and the Undo runner's `record`; it never costs the work.
- **Re-read before writing; a refusal writes nothing.** The row is re-read by exact id (`activityIdFilter`) right before a payback, its Undo and its Redo.
- **Copy (verbatim; constants in `shared/payback-copy.ts` unless named):** `It's a payback »` · `Pick the spending it pays you back for.` · `Filed as a payback under <category> — it takes <$amount> off your <parent or category> spending.` (`money()`: `$14`, `$14.50`) · `Undo it from /changes.` · `It's a payback now, but Wealthfolio didn't file it under <category> — /categorize offers it again.` · `Update the add-on to 1.62.0 or newer first — an older one turns a payback back into a deposit on its next sync. Nothing was changed; once it's updated, paybacks show in the app's Change log too.` · `Undone — it's a deposit again, off <category>.` · `Redone — it's a payback under <category> again.` · `Make every <pattern> deposit a payback` · `Create both rules` · `Couldn't file that — Wealthfolio didn't take it, so it still has no category.` (shared/categorize-menu.ts). Every other string is spelled out in its task.
- **Telegram.** Legacy `Markdown`. `/categorize`'s buttons stay `cz:<generation>:<index>` (≤ 22 bytes; pinned ≤ 64 at `Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER`). Every runner message is RAW and escaped whole with `escapeMarkdown` when rendered, outside any `*…*`/`_…_`; bank, account and category text likewise. No Wealthfolio error text in any message: it is logged. No new glyphs.
- **Tests use a fixed `now`.** Shared and companion: fixed `Date`s and a `now` argument, or `vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] }); vi.setSystemTime(...)` with `vi.useRealTimers()` after. Every new test file also passes with the date forced to 2027-01-01 (Task 10).
- **Imports:** shared sources import siblings WITH `.js`; companion imports `'../../shared/x.js'` and `'./x.js'`.
- **Run the companion suite from `companion/`** (`cd companion && npx vitest run …`), never with `--root`.
- **Gates before calling a task done:** the touched project's tests, plus `nice npm run type-check` when `shared/` changed and `nice npm --prefix companion run build` when `companion/` or `shared/` changed. Final task: `nice npm test`, `(cd companion && nice npx vitest run)`, `nice npm run type-check`, `nice npm --prefix companion run build` — the Mac overheats.
- **Edits are exact.** Every "replace" block below is copied from the file as it stands after the previous tasks (and the earlier edits of the same file in the same task) and occurs exactly once; replace it verbatim (the Edit tool's `old_string` → `new_string`). Apply a file's numbered edits in order. Every "Create" block is the whole file.
- **No version bump, tag or release.** CHANGELOG goes under `[Unreleased]`.
- **Commits** end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

## Review Focus

- **The sync never reverts a payback; it yields when it should** → an overridden row stays a `CREDIT`/`REIMBURSEMENT` sync after sync, beats a transfer keyword, is never paired; money out, a non-cash account, or an override whose year has run out → typed as usual. Pinned in Task 5 (`is never reverted…`, `beats a keyword…`, `yields when the bank turns it into money out…`, `yields on an account that is not cash`, `yields once its year runs out…`).
- **Every refusal writes nothing** → Task 2 (`refuses %s, in its own words, with nothing to write`, `gives every refusal its own words`), Task 3 (`writes nothing for %s`, `writes nothing on a card`, `never overwrites an overrides document it cannot read`), Task 4 (`refuses an Undo when %s, and writes nothing`, `refuses a Redo of a deposit filed since…`), Task 8 (`writes nothing for a row filed elsewhere…`; the index's add-on and sync refusals), Task 9 (`waits for an add-on…`, `never rewrites a transaction-rule list it cannot read`).
- **A write that fails partway leaves an honest state and names it** → Task 3 (`takes the override back when Wealthfolio refuses the re-type…`, `says the override stays…`, `carries on when the re-type landed…`, `says it can’t tell…`, `says it is a payback with no category…`), Task 4 (`a write that fails partway`), Task 9 (`takes the transaction rule back out…`, `says so, and logs the transaction rule for Undo…`).
- **Undo and Redo are an exact round trip** → Task 4 (`is an exact round trip…`), Task 8 (`is undone and redone from /changes through the companion’s Undo host`), Task 9 (`Undo and Redo of the two rules`).
- **Both gates** → Task 1 (`the payback gate (W8)`), Task 3 (`refuses while the installed add-on is %s, naming the app’s Change log…`), Task 4 (`Telegram refuses Undo and Redo with %s…`, `the app points at Telegram, once the companion knows paybacks…`), Task 8 (`refuses while the installed add-on is older…`), Task 9 (`waits for an add-on that reads the rule’s limits…`).
- **No raw Wealthfolio text on any assign failure** → Task 7 (`maps Wealthfolio’s refusal "%s" to the add-on’s own sentence`, `says plainly that Wealthfolio did not take it…`, `a refused assignment (W9)`), Task 3 and Task 4 (`not.toContain` on Wealthfolio's words).
- **W10's preview count matches the rule; both rules logged and undoable** → Task 9 (`counts exactly the waiting deposits the transaction rule types a payback`, `appends the transaction rule, makes Wealthfolio’s rule for paybacks in this account, and logs both`, `Undo and Redo of the two rules`).
- **Telegram's limits** → Task 6 and Task 9 (`keeps every button within 64 bytes, at any generation`); escaping in Task 6 (`shows the runner’s words, escaped…`), Task 8 (`…escaped, with the way on`) and Task 9 (`previews both rules, escaped…`).
- **Dates** → every test pins its clock; Task 10 runs every new test file with the date forced to 2027-01-01.

### Task 1: The `asReimbursement` override and the payback gate

Spec W7's flag and W8's version, as the fixed interface names them. `asReimbursement` joins `TX_OVERRIDE_FLAGS`, so this version reads it strictly (only `true` is a value) and lists it in words — "Payback: <bank text> · <account>" in the app's overrides list, "it's a payback" in a story. `PAYBACK_SINCE = '1.62.0'` mirrors `VENMO_NOTE_SINCE`: the companion's half (`addonUnderstandsPaybacks`) reads `installed_addon_version`, and unknown is false; the app's half (`companionUnderstandsPaybacks`) is true with no companion at all. A 1.61 reader keeps the flag but ignores it (P3-B), which is why both halves wait.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/payback-gate.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/sync-overrides.ts`, `shared/tx-story.ts`, `shared/version.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/sync-overrides.ts
export const TX_OVERRIDE_FLAGS = ['notTransfer', 'keepDuplicate', 'noAutoFile', 'noAmazonLabel', 'noVenmoNote', 'asReimbursement'] as const;
// TxOverride gains asReimbursement?: true; OVERRIDE_FLAG_WORDS.asReimbursement === 'Payback'
// shared/tx-story.ts
TX_OVERRIDE_WORDS.asReimbursement === "it's a payback"
// shared/version.ts
export const PAYBACK_SINCE = '1.62.0';
export function companionUnderstandsPaybacks(companionVersion: string | null): boolean; // null → true
export function addonUnderstandsPaybacks(installedAddonVersion: string | null): boolean; // null → false
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `shared/payback-gate.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  clearTxOverride, emptySyncOverrides, hasTxOverride, overrideRows, parseSyncOverrides, setTxOverride, TX_OVERRIDE_FLAGS,
} from './sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from './transfers.js';
import { buildTxStory } from './tx-story.js';
import {
  addonUnderstandsPaybacks, companionUnderstandsPaybacks, isNewerVersion, PAYBACK_SINCE, SIMPLEFIN_SYNC_VERSION, VENMO_NOTE_SINCE,
} from './version.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-chk', 'TRN-77');

describe('asReimbursement, the payback override', () => {
  it('is a transaction flag: set, read back, listed in words, cleared', () => {
    expect(TX_OVERRIDE_FLAGS).toContain('asReimbursement');
    const o = setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement', NOW, { label: 'VENMO CASHOUT · PNC Checking' });
    expect(hasTxOverride(o, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
    expect(parseSyncOverrides(JSON.stringify(o))).toEqual({ kind: 'ok', overrides: o });
    expect(overrideRows(o).map((r) => r.text)).toEqual(['Payback: VENMO CASHOUT · PNC Checking']);
    expect(clearTxOverride(o, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toEqual(emptySyncOverrides());
  });

  it('is read strictly now that this version knows it: only `true` is a value', () => {
    expect(parseSyncOverrides('{"v":1,"tx":{"k":{"asReimbursement":"yes","at":"2026-10-14T09:00:00Z"}},"accounts":{}}')).toEqual({ kind: 'corrupt' });
  });

  it('is worded in a story as something you told the sync', () => {
    const s = buildTxStory({
      now: NOW,
      row: { activityId: 'a1', note: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', absCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', activityType: 'CREDIT', accountName: 'PNC Checking', accountType: 'CASH' },
      feed: { sfinAccountId: 'sfin-chk' }, partner: null, history: [], overrides: ['asReimbursement', 'noVenmoNote'],
    });
    expect(s.answer.at(-1)).toBe("You told the sync: no Venmo note, it's a payback.");
  });
});

describe('the payback gate (W8)', () => {
  it('is 1.62.0 — raise it to the release that ships paybacks — and never below the Venmo note gate', () => {
    expect(PAYBACK_SINCE).toBe('1.62.0');
    expect(isNewerVersion(VENMO_NOTE_SINCE, PAYBACK_SINCE)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is never above the version this build ships as, once it ships past 1.61.0', () => {
    // A release below PAYBACK_SINCE would refuse its own paybacks forever.
    if (isNewerVersion(SIMPLEFIN_SYNC_VERSION, '1.61.0')) {
      expect(isNewerVersion(PAYBACK_SINCE, SIMPLEFIN_SYNC_VERSION)).toBe(false);
    }
  });

  it('a companion understands paybacks from that release; no companion at all is fine', () => {
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks(PAYBACK_SINCE)).toBe(true);
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks(' 1.62.0 ')).toBe(true);
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks('9.0.0')).toBe(true);
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks('1.61.0')).toBe(false);
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks('garbage')).toBe(false);
    expect(companionUnderstandsPaybacks(null)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('an installed add-on understands them from that release; an unknown one does not', () => {
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks(PAYBACK_SINCE)).toBe(true);
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks('1.62.0\n')).toBe(true);
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks('9.0.0')).toBe(true);
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks('1.61.0')).toBe(false);
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks('garbage')).toBe(false);
    expect(addonUnderstandsPaybacks(null)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Edit 1 of 1 in `shared/version.ts` — replace:

```ts
  return installedAddonVersion !== null && isAtLeastVersion(installedAddonVersion.trim(), VENMO_NOTE_SINCE);
}

/** The companion writes the version in the INSTALLED addon's manifest here
 *  (read off disk), so a browser tab can tell "I am an old bundle — reload me"
 *  apart from "the old zip is still installed — upload the new one". */
```

with:

```ts
  return installedAddonVersion !== null && isAtLeastVersion(installedAddonVersion.trim(), VENMO_NOTE_SINCE);
}

/**
 * The first release whose BOTH halves read `asReimbursement`, the override a
 * payback sets (1.62.0, W6–W8). A reader of 1.61.0 keeps a flag it does not
 * know (it is not corruption, P3-B) but ignores it, so its sync would turn a
 * payback back into a deposit. So Telegram's payback, its Undo and its Redo —
 * and "Make every … deposit a payback" — wait for an INSTALLED add-on this
 * new (refused while the companion cannot tell), and the app's paybacks and
 * their Undo wait for a companion this new. The same pattern as
 * `VENMO_NOTE_SINCE`.
 *
 * Raise to the release that ships this.
 */
export const PAYBACK_SINCE = '1.62.0';

/** The app's half: a companion that reads `asReimbursement`. No companion at
 *  all (null) is true: nothing else is syncing that could ignore it. */
export function companionUnderstandsPaybacks(companionVersion: string | null): boolean {
  if (companionVersion === null) return true;
  return isAtLeastVersion(companionVersion.trim(), PAYBACK_SINCE);
}

/** The companion's half: an installed add-on that reads `asReimbursement`.
 *  Unknown (null — the companion cannot see the addons directory) is false. */
export function addonUnderstandsPaybacks(installedAddonVersion: string | null): boolean {
  return installedAddonVersion !== null && isAtLeastVersion(installedAddonVersion.trim(), PAYBACK_SINCE);
}

/** The companion writes the version in the INSTALLED addon's manifest here
 *  (read off disk), so a browser tab can tell "I am an old bundle — reload me"
 *  apart from "the old zip is still installed — upload the new one". */
```

### Task 2: The pure planner, the copy, and the `payback-filed` change kind

Spec W6 and W7's log, decided purely. `planPayback` takes facts re-read right before a write — the row (`PaybackRow`: the fixes' row plus the stored instant), the feeds that map to its account, the overrides as just read, the category — and returns the writes in order (the override under every feed's key with its label, the re-type, the category, the entry's facts) or a refusal, each in its own words: gone, managed by the sync, not from the bank feed, half of a linked transfer, a transfer, pending, not a cash account, already a payback (by type or override), not a deposit, an unreadable overrides document. The re-type (`paybackRetype`, in the leaf `shared/payback-row.ts` so the Undo planner can share it) keeps the account, amount, currency and note, writes the stored instant back unchanged, and states the subtype — `''` clears it. `paybackFiledChange` builds the fixed interface's entry; it is shown under Categories ("Money in, no category → Payback under Restaurants"). Every payback sentence goes in `shared/payback-copy.ts` now, so later tasks only import it.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/payback-copy.ts`, `shared/payback-row.ts`, `shared/payback.test.ts`, `shared/payback.ts`
- Modify: `shared/change-entries.test.ts`, `shared/change-entries.ts`, `shared/change-view.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`asReimbursement`).
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/payback-row.ts
export interface PaybackRow extends FixRow { instant: string }
export const isPaybackRow: (row: Pick<FixRow, 'type' | 'subtype'>) => boolean;
export function paybackRetype(row: PaybackRow, activityType: string, subtype: string): ActivityWrite;
// shared/payback.ts
export function paybackRowOf(raw: unknown, accountType: string): PaybackRow | null;
export interface PaybackFacts { row: PaybackRow | null; feeds: string[]; overrides: LoadedSyncOverrides; category: { id: string; name: string } }
export interface PaybackWrites { txKeys: string[]; label: string; update: ActivityWrite; assign: { activityId: string; categoryId: string }; entry: PaybackFiledFacts }
export type PaybackPlan = { ok: true; writes: PaybackWrites } | { ok: false; reason: string };
export function planPayback(f: PaybackFacts): PaybackPlan;
// shared/change-entries.ts — ChangeKind gains 'payback-filed' (CHANGE_KINDS: 20)
export interface PaybackFiledFacts {
  account: { sfinId: string; wfId: string; name: string }; txKeys: string[]; activityId: string; description: string;
  amountCents: number; date: string; subtypeBefore: string | null; categoryId: string | null; categoryName: string | null;
}
export function paybackFiledChange(f: PaybackFiledFacts): ChangeDraft;
// shared/payback-copy.ts — every PAYBACK_* constant and payback*() sentence the later tasks import
```

**Create `shared/payback.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { paybackFiledChange, reversalChange, stampChange } from './change-entries.js';
import { changeBeforeAfter, changeGroupOf } from './change-view.js';
import { paybackRowOf, planPayback, type PaybackFacts } from './payback.js';
import { isPaybackRow, paybackRetype, type PaybackRow } from './payback-row.js';
import {
  PAYBACK_ALREADY, PAYBACK_GONE, PAYBACK_LINKED, PAYBACK_MANAGED, PAYBACK_NOT_CASH, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED,
  PAYBACK_PENDING, PAYBACK_TRANSFER, paybackDone,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import { emptySyncOverrides, setTxOverride, SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE } from './sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from './transfers.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-chk', 'TRN-77');

/** Wealthfolio's ActivityDetails for a Venmo payback that landed as a plain deposit. */
const DETAILS = {
  id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', accountName: 'PNC Checking', activityType: 'DEPOSIT', date: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z',
  amount: '14', fee: '0', currency: 'USD', comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: null, sourceGroupId: null,
};
const deposit = (over: Partial<PaybackRow> = {}): PaybackRow => ({ ...paybackRowOf(DETAILS, 'CASH')!, ...over });
const facts = (over: Partial<PaybackFacts> = {}): PaybackFacts => ({
  row: deposit(),
  feeds: ['sfin-chk'],
  overrides: { overrides: emptySyncOverrides(), unreadable: false },
  category: { id: 'cat-rest', name: 'Restaurants' },
  ...over,
});

describe('paybackRowOf', () => {
  it('reads the fixes’ row and keeps the stored instant exactly', () => {
    const row = paybackRowOf(DETAILS, 'cash')!;
    expect(row).toMatchObject({
      id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', accountType: 'CASH', type: 'DEPOSIT', date: '2026-10-09', absCents: 1400,
      currency: 'USD', notes: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: null, grouped: false, instant: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z',
    });
    expect(paybackRowOf({ ...DETAILS, date: '2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00' }, 'CASH')!.instant).toBe('2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00');
  });

  it('is null for a row the fixes cannot read', () => {
    expect(paybackRowOf({ ...DETAILS, currency: undefined }, 'CASH')).toBeNull();
    expect(paybackRowOf('nope', 'CASH')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('knows a payback by its type and subtype, in any case', () => {
    expect(isPaybackRow({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'reimbursement' })).toBe(true);
    expect(isPaybackRow({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: null })).toBe(false);
    expect(isPaybackRow({ type: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' })).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('planPayback', () => {
  it('re-types the deposit to a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT, at its stored instant, and files it', () => {
    const plan = planPayback(facts({ feeds: ['sfin-chk', 'sfin-old'] }));
    expect(plan).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      writes: {
        txKeys: [KEY, accountTxKey('sfin-old', 'TRN-77')],
        label: 'VENMO CASHOUT · PNC Checking',
        update: {
          id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT', activityDate: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z',
          symbol: { symbol: '$CASH-USD' }, amount: 14, currency: 'USD', comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT',
        },
        assign: { activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' },
        entry: {
          account: { sfinId: 'sfin-chk', wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' },
          txKeys: [KEY, accountTxKey('sfin-old', 'TRN-77')],
          activityId: 'act-7', description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', subtypeBefore: null,
          categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
        },
      },
    });
  });

  it('keeps a deposit’s own subtype in the entry, so Undo can put it back', () => {
    const plan = planPayback(facts({ row: deposit({ subtype: 'BONUS' }) }));
    expect(plan.ok && plan.writes.entry.subtypeBefore).toBe('BONUS');
  });

  it.each<[string, Partial<PaybackFacts>, string]>([
    ['a row that is gone', { row: null }, PAYBACK_GONE],
    ['a held transfer', { row: deposit({ notes: '↔️ In-transit transfer · ZELLE FROM MOM · TRN-77' }) }, PAYBACK_MANAGED],
    ['a starting balance', { row: deposit({ notes: 'Starting balance · sfin-chk' }) }, PAYBACK_MANAGED],
    ['a correction', { row: deposit({ notes: 'Balance adjustment · sfin-chk · 2026-10-09' }) }, PAYBACK_MANAGED],
    ['a row added by hand', { row: deposit({ notes: 'Dinner money' }) }, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED],
    ['an account no feed maps to', { feeds: [] }, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED],
    ['half of a linked transfer', { row: deposit({ grouped: true }) }, PAYBACK_LINKED],
    ['a transfer', { row: deposit({ type: 'TRANSFER_IN' }) }, PAYBACK_TRANSFER],
    ['a pending row', { row: deposit({ notes: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77 · pending' }) }, PAYBACK_PENDING],
    ['a card', { row: deposit({ accountType: 'CREDIT_CARD' }) }, PAYBACK_NOT_CASH],
    ['a row that is already a payback', { row: deposit({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' }) }, PAYBACK_ALREADY],
    ['a row whose override says so already', {
      overrides: { overrides: setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement', NOW), unreadable: false },
    }, PAYBACK_ALREADY],
    ['interest', { row: deposit({ type: 'INTEREST' }) }, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT],
    ['a bare credit', { row: deposit({ type: 'CREDIT' }) }, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT],
    ['an unreadable overrides document', { overrides: { overrides: emptySyncOverrides(), unreadable: true } }, SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE],
  ])('refuses %s, in its own words, with nothing to write', (_name, over, reason) => {
    expect(planPayback(facts(over))).toEqual({ ok: false, reason });
  });

  it('gives every refusal its own words', () => {
    const words = [PAYBACK_GONE, PAYBACK_MANAGED, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED, PAYBACK_LINKED, PAYBACK_TRANSFER, PAYBACK_PENDING, PAYBACK_NOT_CASH,
      PAYBACK_ALREADY, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT, SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE];
    expect(new Set(words).size).toBe(words.length);
  });

  it('clears a subtype with an explicit empty one, and keeps everything else the row had', () => {
    expect(paybackRetype(deposit({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' }), 'DEPOSIT', '')).toEqual({
      id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'DEPOSIT', activityDate: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z',
      symbol: { symbol: '$CASH-USD' }, amount: 14, currency: 'USD', comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: '',
    });
  });
});

describe('the payback-filed entry', () => {
  const draft = paybackFiledChange({
    account: { sfinId: 'sfin-chk', wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' }, txKeys: [KEY], activityId: 'act-7',
    description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', subtypeBefore: null, categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
  });

  it('carries the interface the app’s paybacks read (W11): before, after, subject', () => {
    expect(draft).toMatchObject({
      actor: 'you', kind: 'payback-filed', undoable: true,
      account: { sfinId: 'sfin-chk', wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' },
      subject: { txKeys: [KEY], activityIds: ['act-7'], description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', categoryName: 'Restaurants' },
      summary: 'Filed VENMO CASHOUT as a payback under Restaurants',
    });
    expect(draft.before).toEqual({ activityType: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: null, categoryId: null });
    expect(draft.after).toEqual({
      activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', accountId: 'wf-chk',
    });
  });

  it('says so when Wealthfolio did not take the category', () => {
    const bare = paybackFiledChange({
      account: { sfinId: 'sfin-chk', wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' }, txKeys: [KEY], activityId: 'act-7',
      description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', subtypeBefore: null, categoryId: null, categoryName: null,
    });
    expect(bare.summary).toBe('Made VENMO CASHOUT a payback');
    expect(bare.subject).not.toHaveProperty('categoryName');
  });

  it('is shown under Categories, before and after in words, both ways', () => {
    const entry = stampChange(draft, 'companion', NOW);
    expect(changeGroupOf('payback-filed')).toBe('categories');
    expect(changeBeforeAfter(entry)).toBe('Money in, no category → Payback under Restaurants');
    expect(changeBeforeAfter(stampChange(reversalChange(entry, 'undo'), 'companion', NOW))).toBe('Payback under Restaurants → Money in, no category');
  });

  it('words the filing as the spec does', () => {
    expect(paybackDone('Restaurants', 'Food & Dining', 1400)).toBe('Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.');
    expect(paybackDone('Entertainment', 'Entertainment', 1450)).toBe('Filed as a payback under Entertainment — it takes $14.50 off your Entertainment spending.');
  });
});
```

```ts
export interface PaybackFiledFacts {
  account: { sfinId: string; wfId: string; name: string };
  /** The row's key under every feed of its account: the override went on each. */
  txKeys: string[];
  activityId: string;
  /** Bank text, raw. */
  description: string;
  /** Money in: positive cents. */
  amountCents: number;
  date: string;
  /** The deposit's subtype before (null: none). Undo puts it back. */
  subtypeBefore: string | null;
  /** The spending category it was filed under; null when Wealthfolio did not take the filing. */
  categoryId: string | null;
  categoryName: string | null;
}

/**
 * A deposit you filed as a payback (1.62.0, W6): re-typed to a CREDIT marked
 * REIMBURSEMENT, kept that way by the `asReimbursement` override, and filed
 * under a spending category. Undo: the override off, the row a DEPOSIT again
 * with its old subtype, the category off. Redo: all three again.
 */
export function paybackFiledChange(f: PaybackFiledFacts): ChangeDraft {
  return {
    actor: 'you',
    kind: 'payback-filed',
    account: { ...f.account },
    subject: {
      txKeys: [...f.txKeys],
      activityIds: [f.activityId],
      description: f.description,
      amountCents: f.amountCents,
      date: f.date,
      ...(f.categoryName ? { categoryName: f.categoryName } : {}),
    },
    summary: f.categoryName
      ? `Filed ${what(f.description)} as a payback under ${f.categoryName}`
      : `Made ${what(f.description)} a payback`,
    why: 'You said it pays you back',
    before: { activityType: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: f.subtypeBefore, categoryId: null },
    after: {
      activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', categoryId: f.categoryId, categoryName: f.categoryName, accountId: f.account.wfId,
    },
    undoable: true,
  };
}
```

**Create `shared/payback-copy.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/payback-copy.ts
 *
 * Every sentence the payback feature says (1.62.0, W6–W10), in one place:
 * Telegram's /categorize, the change log's Undo and Redo, and "Make every …
 * deposit a payback". RAW text: bank, account and category names go in as
 * they are, and Telegram escapes the whole message when it renders it, outside
 * any bold or italic — no sentence here uses Markdown. A leaf module, so the
 * planner, the filing runner and the change log's runner can share it without
 * importing one another.
 */
import { money } from './telegram.js';

// ── The menu ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** The switch on a deposit's category screen (it read "Spending categories »"). */
export const PAYBACK_SWITCH = "It's a payback »";
/** The line over the payback grid. */
export const PAYBACK_PICK = 'Pick the spending it pays you back for.';

// ── Filing ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
/** "Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending." */
export function paybackDone(categoryName: string, spendingName: string, absCents: number): string {
  return `Filed as a payback under ${categoryName} — it takes ${money(absCents / 100)} off your ${spendingName} spending.`;
}
export const PAYBACK_UNDO_HINT = 'Undo it from /changes.';
export const PAYBACK_NO_UNDO = "The change log couldn't be written, so there's no Undo for it.";
export const paybackUnfiled = (categoryName: string): string =>
  `It's a payback now, but Wealthfolio didn't file it under ${categoryName} — /categorize offers it again.`;

export const PAYBACK_GONE = 'That transaction is gone — nothing was changed.';
export const PAYBACK_MANAGED = "The sync manages this row (a held transfer, a starting balance or a correction), so it can't be a payback.";
export const PAYBACK_NOT_FEED = 'Only a transaction from your bank feed can be a payback here — nothing was changed.';
export const PAYBACK_LINKED = "It's half of a linked transfer, so it can't be a payback — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_TRANSFER = "It's recorded as a transfer, so it can't be a payback — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_PENDING = "It's still pending — file it as a payback once it posts.";
export const PAYBACK_NOT_CASH = 'Only money into a bank account can be a payback — on a card, Wealthfolio already takes a credit off spending.';
export const PAYBACK_ALREADY = "It's already a payback — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT = 'Only a deposit can be a payback — nothing was changed.';
/** Telegram, while the installed add-on is older than PAYBACK_SINCE or unknown (W8). */
export const PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON = "Update the add-on to 1.62.0 or newer first — an older one turns a payback back into a deposit on its next sync. Nothing was changed; once it's updated, paybacks show in the app's Change log too.";
export const PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE = "Couldn't reach Wealthfolio — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_RUNNING = 'Already being filed — try again in a moment.';
export const PAYBACK_UNAVAILABLE = "Paybacks aren't available here — nothing was changed.";

export const PAYBACK_OVERRIDE_FAILED = "Couldn't save the payback setting — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_UPDATE_REFUSED = "Wealthfolio didn't take the change, so it's still a deposit — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_UPDATE_STUCK = "Wealthfolio didn't take the change, so it's still a deposit — but the payback setting couldn't be taken back, so the next sync makes it a payback with no category, and /categorize offers it then.";
export const PAYBACK_UPDATE_UNSURE = "Couldn't tell whether Wealthfolio took the change. The payback setting stays, so the next sync makes it a payback; if it has no category then, /categorize offers it.";

// ── Undo and Redo ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
export const paybackUndone = (categoryName: string | null): string =>
  `Undone — it's a deposit again${categoryName ? `, off ${categoryName}` : ''}.`;
export const paybackRedone = (categoryName: string | null): string =>
  `Redone — it's a payback${categoryName ? ` under ${categoryName}` : ''} again.`;
export const PAYBACK_UNDO_UNCHANGED = "Couldn't undo it — Wealthfolio didn't take the change. It's still a payback, and nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_UNDO_STUCK = "Couldn't undo it — Wealthfolio didn't take the change, and the payback setting couldn't be put back, so the next sync turns it into a deposit that is still filed under its category. Take that off in Wealthfolio then.";
export const paybackUndoHalf = (categoryName: string): string =>
  `Couldn't finish the undo — it's a deposit again, but it's still filed under ${categoryName}. Take that off in Wealthfolio.`;
export const PAYBACK_REDO_UNCHANGED = "Couldn't redo it — Wealthfolio didn't take the change. It's still a deposit, and nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_REDO_STUCK = "Couldn't redo it — Wealthfolio didn't take the change, and the payback setting couldn't be taken back, so the next sync makes it a payback with no category, and /categorize offers it then.";
export const paybackRedoHalf = (categoryName: string): string =>
  `Couldn't finish the redo — it's a payback again, but Wealthfolio didn't file it under ${categoryName}; /categorize offers it.`;

// ── "Make every … deposit a payback" (W10) ───────────────────────────────
export const paybackRulesButton = (pattern: string): string => `Make every ${pattern} deposit a payback`;
export function paybackRulesPreview(pattern: string, accountName: string, categoryName: string, catches: number): string {
  return [
    `Make every ${pattern} deposit a payback?`,
    `Money into ${accountName} whose description contains "${pattern}" becomes a payback under ${categoryName}: `
      + 'the sync marks it when it imports, and Wealthfolio files it. Undo either rule from /changes.',
    catches > 0
      ? `Right now that also catches ${catches} other deposit${catches === 1 ? '' : 's'} waiting for a category.`
      : 'Nothing else waiting for a category matches right now.',
  ].join('\n');
}
export const PAYBACK_RULES_CREATE = 'Create both rules';
export const paybackRulesDone = (pattern: string, accountName: string, categoryName: string): string =>
  `Done — every ${pattern} deposit into ${accountName} is a payback under ${categoryName} from now on. Undo either rule from /changes.`;
export const PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST = 'Both rules are already there — nothing was added.';
export const PAYBACK_RULES_SAVE_FAILED = "Couldn't save the transaction rule — nothing was changed.";
export const PAYBACK_RULES_REFUSED = "Wealthfolio didn't take the category rule, so neither rule was made.";
export const paybackRulesStuck = (pattern: string): string =>
  `Wealthfolio didn't take the category rule, and the transaction rule couldn't be taken back — new ${pattern} deposits become paybacks with no category. Undo the transaction rule from /changes.`;
```

**Create `shared/payback-row.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/payback-row.ts
 *
 * A row as a payback reads it, and the one write that re-types it (1.62.0,
 * W6–W7) — shared by the planner (shared/payback.ts) and by the change log's
 * Undo and Redo (shared/change-undo.ts), which must not import the planner:
 * the planner reaches the change log's runner through the fixes' reader. Pure.
 */
import { REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE } from './cash-flow-bucket.js';
import type { FixRow } from './fix-row.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from './sync-host.js';

/** The fixes' row (shared/fix-row.ts), plus the stored instant exactly as the
 *  host reported it. */
export interface PaybackRow extends FixRow {
  /** `2026-10-09T12:00:00Z`, `2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00`, or a bare day from a
   *  host that sends one: written back unchanged on a retype. */
  instant: string;
}

/** Already a payback: a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT (any case, as Wealthfolio
 *  canonicalizes it). */
export const isPaybackRow = (row: Pick<FixRow, 'type' | 'subtype'>): boolean =>
  row.type === 'CREDIT' && (row.subtype ?? '').trim().toUpperCase() === REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE;

/**
 * The row re-typed, as the bulk endpoint's update: the same account, date
 * (the stored instant), amount, currency and note, the cash asset by symbol
 * (as the sync writes every non-transfer row), and `subtype` stated — an
 * explicit `''` clears it (Wealthfolio 3.8–3.10 stores none for an empty
 * subtype on an update; an omitted one is kept). Fee, metadata and the review
 * flag are left out, so they stay as stored.
 */
export function paybackRetype(row: PaybackRow, activityType: string, subtype: string): ActivityWrite {
  return {
    id: row.id,
    accountId: row.accountId,
    activityType,
    activityDate: row.instant,
    symbol: { symbol: `$CASH-${row.currency}` },
    amount: row.absCents / 100,
    currency: row.currency,
    comment: row.notes,
    subtype,
  };
}
```

**Create `shared/payback.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * shared/payback.ts
 *
 * Paybacks (1.62.0, W6–W10): a deposit that pays you back for spending — a
 * friend's Venmo for dinner — filed against that spending. Since 3.9,
 * Wealthfolio decides which categories a row can take from the row's type: a
 * cash DEPOSIT is income, and only a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT (REFUND,
 * REBATE) comes off spending (`classify_activity`,
 * crates/spending/src/activity_classification.rs:98-130 at 3.8.0/3.9.0,
 * :100-132 at 3.10.0). So filing a payback re-types the row before it files
 * it:
 *
 *  1. the `asReimbursement` override, under every feed of the row's account,
 *     through `editSyncOverrides` (which re-reads right before it writes), so
 *     the sync keeps the row a payback instead of reverting it (W7);
 *  2. the row: a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT, everything else as stored — its
 *     date written back as the very instant Wealthfolio holds, so a retype
 *     never moves a row to another day;
 *  3. the spending category;
 *  4. a `payback-filed` change-log entry, whose Undo reverses all three.
 *
 * `planPayback` decides from facts re-read right before writing; a refusal
 * writes nothing and says why in its own words. Pure.
 */
import { isCorrectionNote, isPendingNote, isPlaceholderNote, isStartingBalanceNote } from './audit-ledger.js';
import { REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE } from './cash-flow-bucket.js';
import type { PaybackFiledFacts } from './change-entries.js';
import { fixRowOf } from './fix-row.js';
import {
  PAYBACK_ALREADY, PAYBACK_GONE, PAYBACK_LINKED, PAYBACK_MANAGED, PAYBACK_NOT_CASH, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED,
  PAYBACK_PENDING, PAYBACK_TRANSFER,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import { isPaybackRow, paybackRetype, type PaybackRow } from './payback-row.js';
import { descriptionFromComment, isTransferType, txIdFromComment } from './sync-core.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from './sync-host.js';
import { hasTxOverride, SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE, type LoadedSyncOverrides } from './sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from './transfers.js';

/** An ActivityDetails row as a payback reads it; null when the fixes' reader
 *  refuses it (no id, account, type, date or currency). */
export function paybackRowOf(raw: unknown, accountType: string): PaybackRow | null {
  const row = fixRowOf(raw, accountType);
  if (!row) return null;
  const at = (raw as { date?: unknown }).date;
  return { ...row, instant: typeof at === 'string' && at.trim() !== '' ? at.trim() : row.date };
}

/** What `planPayback` decides from, all re-read right before the write. */
export interface PaybackFacts {
  /** The row by exact id; null when gone. */
  row: PaybackRow | null;
  /** The SimpleFin accounts that sync into the row's account ([] when none). */
  feeds: string[];
  /** `loadSyncOverrides`, as just read. */
  overrides: LoadedSyncOverrides;
  /** The spending category picked. */
  category: { id: string; name: string };
}

/** What a payback writes, in order. */
export interface PaybackWrites {
  /** The row's key under every feed of its account: `asReimbursement` goes on each. */
  txKeys: string[];
  /** The overrides list's words for it: "<bank text> · <account>". */
  label: string;
  /** The row as a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT. */
  update: ActivityWrite;
  /** The spending category. */
  assign: { activityId: string; categoryId: string };
  /** The change-log entry's facts (`paybackFiledChange`). */
  entry: PaybackFiledFacts;
}

export type PaybackPlan = { ok: true; writes: PaybackWrites } | { ok: false; reason: string };

const no = (reason: string): PaybackPlan => ({ ok: false, reason });

/**
 * Decide a payback. Refused, each in its own words and with nothing written:
 * a row that is gone; one the sync manages (a held transfer, a starting
 * balance, a correction); one not from the bank feed (no tx id, or an account
 * no feed maps to — the override needs a key the sync reads); half of a
 * linked transfer; a transfer; a pending row (SimpleFin often gives it a new
 * id when it posts, which the override would not follow); a row outside a
 * cash account (on a card every CREDIT already comes off spending); one that
 * is already a payback (by its type or its override); anything but a
 * DEPOSIT; and an overrides document that cannot be read (never overwritten).
 */
export function planPayback(f: PaybackFacts): PaybackPlan {
  const row = f.row;
  if (!row) return no(PAYBACK_GONE);
  if (isPlaceholderNote(row.notes) || isStartingBalanceNote(row.notes) || isCorrectionNote(row.notes)) return no(PAYBACK_MANAGED);
  const txId = txIdFromComment(row.notes);
  if (!txId || f.feeds.length === 0) return no(PAYBACK_NOT_FEED);
  if (row.grouped) return no(PAYBACK_LINKED);
  if (isTransferType(row.type)) return no(PAYBACK_TRANSFER);
  if (isPendingNote(row.notes)) return no(PAYBACK_PENDING);
  if (row.accountType !== 'CASH') return no(PAYBACK_NOT_CASH);
  const txKeys = f.feeds.map((sfin) => accountTxKey(sfin, txId));
  if (isPaybackRow(row) || txKeys.some((k) => hasTxOverride(f.overrides.overrides, k, 'asReimbursement'))) return no(PAYBACK_ALREADY);
  if (row.type !== 'DEPOSIT') return no(PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT);
  if (f.overrides.unreadable) return no(SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE);
  const description = descriptionFromComment(row.notes);
  const what = description || 'a transaction';
  return {
    ok: true,
    writes: {
      txKeys,
      label: row.accountName ? `${what} · ${row.accountName}` : what,
      update: paybackRetype(row, 'CREDIT', REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE),
      assign: { activityId: row.id, categoryId: f.category.id },
      entry: {
        account: { sfinId: f.feeds[0], wfId: row.accountId, name: row.accountName },
        txKeys,
        activityId: row.id,
        description,
        amountCents: row.absCents,
        date: row.date,
        subtypeBefore: row.subtype,
        categoryId: f.category.id,
        categoryName: f.category.name,
      },
    },
  };
}
```

---

### Task 3: Filing a payback: the runner

W6's order, on a host. `filePayback` gates first (the host's `gate`: on the companion, an installed add-on of 1.62.0 — `addonPaybackGate`, unknown refuses), re-reads the row, its feeds and the overrides, plans, then writes: (1) the override, inside `editSyncOverrides`, noting which keys this run added; (2) the re-type — refused, it reads the row again: still a deposit, it takes back only what it added ("nothing was changed"), and when that write fails too it says the next sync makes it a payback; can't tell, the override stays and says so; a payback after all (a lost response), it carries on; (3) the category — refused, it is a payback with no category, which `/categorize` offers again; (4) the entry, which never costs the payback (without it the message says there is no Undo). Wealthfolio's text goes to `io.log`, never into the message. A second tap on the same row while one runs is refused.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/payback-file.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/payback.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`planPayback`, `paybackRowOf`, `PaybackRow`, `paybackFiledChange`, the copy); Task 1 (`addonUnderstandsPaybacks`).
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/payback.ts
export interface PaybackIo {
  gate(): Promise<string | null>; read(activityId: string): Promise<PaybackRow | null>; feeds(wfAccountId: string): Promise<string[]>;
  secrets: { get: SecretGet; set: SecretSet }; update(write: ActivityWrite): Promise<void>;
  assign(activityId: string, categoryId: string): Promise<void>; record(draft: ChangeDraft): Promise<void>; log(msg: string): void;
}
export interface PaybackChoice { activityId: string; categoryId: string; categoryName: string; parentName: string | null }
export type PaybackOutcome = { kind: 'filed' | 'partial' | 'refused'; message: string };
export async function addonPaybackGate(get: SecretGet): Promise<string | null>;
export async function filePayback(choice: PaybackChoice, io: PaybackIo, now: Date): Promise<PaybackOutcome>;
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';

  PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON, PAYBACK_UPDATE_REFUSED, PAYBACK_UPDATE_STUCK, PAYBACK_UPDATE_UNSURE,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import {

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-chk', 'TRN-77');
const OTHER = accountTxKey('sfin-chk', 'TRN-1');

const RESTAURANTS: PaybackChoice = { activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', parentName: 'Food & Dining' };

/** Wealthfolio and the add-on's secrets, in memory, every call in order. */
function world(over: { row?: Record<string, unknown> | null; secrets?: Record<string, string>; io?: Partial<PaybackIo> } = {}) {
  let row: PaybackRow | null = over.row === null ? null : paybackRowOf({ ...DETAILS, ...(over.row ?? {}) }, 'CASH');
  const data = new Map(Object.entries({ [INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY]: '1.62.0', ...(over.secrets ?? {}) }));
  const calls: string[] = [];

    feeds: vi.fn(async (wf: string) => { calls.push(`feeds ${wf}`); return ['sfin-chk']; }),

      row = paybackRowOf({ ...DETAILS, ...(over.row ?? {}), activityType: w.activityType, subtype: w.subtype || null, date: w.activityDate }, 'CASH');
    }),
    assign: vi.fn(async (id: string, cat: string) => { calls.push(`assign ${id} ${cat}`); }),
    record: vi.fn(async (d: ChangeDraft) => { calls.push(`record ${d.kind}`); logged.push(stampChange(d, 'companion', NOW)); }),
    log: (m: string) => { logs.push(m); },
    ...(over.io ?? {}),
  };
  const overrides = () => {
    const p = parseSyncOverrides(data.get(SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY));
    return p.kind === 'ok' ? p.overrides : p.kind === 'absent' ? emptySyncOverrides() : null;
  };
  return { io, data, calls, logged, logs, overrides, row: () => row };
}

describe('filePayback', () => {
  it('sets the override, re-types the row, files it, logs it — in that order — and says so as the spec does', async () => {
    const w = world();
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: 'filed',
      message: 'Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.',
    });
    expect(w.calls).toEqual([
      'read act-7', 'feeds wf-chk', `set ${SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY}`, 'update CREDIT/REIMBURSEMENT', 'assign act-7 cat-rest', 'record payback-filed',
    ]);
    expect(w.overrides()!.tx[KEY]).toEqual({ asReimbursement: true, at: NOW.toISOString(), label: 'VENMO CASHOUT · PNC Checking' });
    expect(w.io.update).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ id: 'act-7', activityDate: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z', amount: 14 }));
    expect(w.logged[0]).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'payback-filed', actor: 'you', subject: { txKeys: [KEY], activityIds: ['act-7'] },
      after: { activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', accountId: 'wf-chk' },
    });
  });

  it('keeps every other override in the document', async () => {
    const w = world({ secrets: { [SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY]: JSON.stringify(setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), OTHER, 'notTransfer', NOW)) } });
    await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW);
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, OTHER, 'notTransfer')).toBe(true);
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each<[string, Record<string, string>]>([
    ['an add-on older than 1.62.0', { [INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY]: '1.61.0' }],
    ['an add-on the companion cannot see', { [INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY]: '' }],
  ])('refuses while the installed add-on is %s, naming the app’s Change log, and reads and writes nothing', async (_name, secrets) => {
    const w = world({ secrets });
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON });
    expect(PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON).toContain("the app's Change log");
    expect(w.calls).toEqual([]);
  });

  it.each<[string, Record<string, unknown>, string]>([
    ['a pending row', { comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77 · pending' }, PAYBACK_PENDING],
    ['half of a linked transfer', { sourceGroupId: 'wf-transfer-1' }, PAYBACK_LINKED],
  ])('writes nothing for %s', async (_name, row, message) => {
    const w = world({ row });
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message });
    expect(w.calls).toEqual(['read act-7', 'feeds wf-chk']);
  });

  it('writes nothing on a card', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.read = async () => paybackRowOf(DETAILS, 'CREDIT_CARD');
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_NOT_CASH });
    expect(w.calls).toEqual(['feeds wf-chk']);
  });

  it('never overwrites an overrides document it cannot read', async () => {
    const w = world({ secrets: { [SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY]: '{oops' } });
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE });
    expect(w.data.get(SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY)).toBe('{oops');
    expect(w.io.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('says it couldn’t reach Wealthfolio when the read fails, and writes nothing', async () => {
    const w = world({ io: { read: async () => { throw new Error('ECONNREFUSED'); } } });
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE });
    expect(w.io.secrets.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('stops at an override it could not save', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.secrets.set = async () => { throw new Error('500'); };
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_OVERRIDE_FAILED });
    expect(w.io.update).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('takes the override back when Wealthfolio refuses the re-type, so nothing changed', async () => {
    const w = world({ secrets: { [SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY]: JSON.stringify(setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), OTHER, 'notTransfer', NOW)) } });
    w.io.update = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('400 - nope'); });
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_REFUSED });
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, OTHER, 'notTransfer')).toBe(true);
    expect(w.io.assign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(w.io.record).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(w.logs.join('\n')).toContain('400 - nope');
  });

  it('says the override stays when it cannot take it back', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.update = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('400'); });
    let writes = 0;
    const set = w.io.secrets.set;
    w.io.secrets.set = async (k, v) => { writes += 1; if (writes > 1) throw new Error('500'); await set(k, v); };
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'partial', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_STUCK });
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('carries on when the re-type landed but its answer was lost', async () => {
    const w = world();
    const update = w.io.update;
    w.io.update = vi.fn(async (wr) => { await update(wr); throw new Error('socket hang up'); });
    expect((await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).kind).toBe('filed');
    expect(w.io.assign).toHaveBeenCalledWith('act-7', 'cat-rest');
  });

  it('says it can’t tell, and keeps the override, when the read after a failed re-type fails too', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.update = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('socket hang up'); });
    let reads = 0;
    const read = w.io.read;
    w.io.read = async (id) => { reads += 1; if (reads > 1) throw new Error('ECONNRESET'); return read(id); };
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'partial', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_UNSURE });
    expect(hasTxOverride(w.overrides()!, KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('says it is a payback with no category when Wealthfolio refuses the filing — never Wealthfolio’s words — and logs it so Undo works', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.assign = vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error(RAW); });
    const out = await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW);
    expect(out).toEqual({
      kind: 'partial',
      message: "It's a payback now, but Wealthfolio didn't file it under Restaurants — /categorize offers it again.\nUndo it from /changes.",
    });
    expect(out.message).not.toContain('Income activities');
    expect(w.logs.join('\n')).toContain('Income activities');
    expect(w.logged[0]).toMatchObject({ summary: 'Made VENMO CASHOUT a payback', after: { categoryId: null, categoryName: null } });
  });

  it('still files when the change log cannot be written, and says there is no Undo', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.record = async () => { throw new Error('quota'); };
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({
      kind: 'filed',
      message: `Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\n${PAYBACK_NO_UNDO}`,
    });
  });

  it('files a second tap on the same row once', async () => {
    const w = world();
    let release!: () => void;
    const gate = new Promise<void>((r) => { release = r; });
    const read = w.io.read;
    w.io.read = async (id) => { await gate; return read(id); };
    const first = filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW);
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, w.io, NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_RUNNING });
    release();
    expect((await first).kind).toBe('filed');
  });
});
```

```ts
import { isCorrectionNote, isPendingNote, isPlaceholderNote, isStartingBalanceNote } from './audit-ledger.js';
import { REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE } from './cash-flow-bucket.js';
import { paybackFiledChange, type ChangeDraft, type PaybackFiledFacts } from './change-entries.js';

import {
  PAYBACK_ALREADY, PAYBACK_GONE, PAYBACK_LINKED, PAYBACK_MANAGED, PAYBACK_NO_UNDO, PAYBACK_NOT_CASH, PAYBACK_NOT_DEPOSIT, PAYBACK_NOT_FEED,
  PAYBACK_OVERRIDE_FAILED, PAYBACK_PENDING, PAYBACK_RUNNING, PAYBACK_TRANSFER, PAYBACK_UNDO_HINT, PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE, PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON,
  PAYBACK_UPDATE_REFUSED, PAYBACK_UPDATE_STUCK, PAYBACK_UPDATE_UNSURE, paybackDone, paybackUnfiled,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import { isPaybackRow, paybackRetype, type PaybackRow } from './payback-row.js';
import type { SecretGet, SecretSet } from './subscription-prefs.js';
import { descriptionFromComment, isTransferType, txIdFromComment } from './sync-core.js';
import type { ActivityWrite } from './sync-host.js';

  SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE, type LoadedSyncOverrides,
} from './sync-overrides.js';
```

```ts
// ── Filing: the runner ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

/** What a host lends `filePayback`. */
export interface PaybackIo {
  /** Why this host may not file a payback now (a version gate, W8), or null. Never rejects. */
  gate(): Promise<string | null>;
  /** The row by exact id, now; null when gone. Rejects when unreachable. */
  read(activityId: string): Promise<PaybackRow | null>;
  /** The SimpleFin accounts that sync into this Wealthfolio account. Rejects when unreadable. */
  feeds(wfAccountId: string): Promise<string[]>;
  secrets: { get: SecretGet; set: SecretSet };
  /** One update through the bulk endpoint; rejects when Wealthfolio refuses it. */
  update(write: ActivityWrite): Promise<void>;
  /** Files the row under a spending category; rejects when Wealthfolio refuses. */
  assign(activityId: string, categoryId: string): Promise<void>;
  /** Appends to the change log through the host's own writer. */
  record(draft: ChangeDraft): Promise<void>;
  log(msg: string): void;
}

/** The tap: the row, and the spending category picked. */
export interface PaybackChoice {
  activityId: string;
  categoryId: string;
  categoryName: string;
  /** The category's parent, whose spending it comes off (`Food & Dining` for
   *  Restaurants); null for a top-level category. */
  parentName: string | null;
}

/** `filed`: all of it. `partial`: something changed, but not all of it — the
 *  message says what stands. `refused`: nothing was written. */
export type PaybackOutcome =
  | { kind: 'filed'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'partial'; message: string }
  | { kind: 'refused'; message: string };

/** The companion's gate (W8): an INSTALLED add-on of PAYBACK_SINCE or newer.
 *  Unknown — nothing written, or the read failed — refuses too. */
export async function addonPaybackGate(get: SecretGet): Promise<string | null> {
  let installed: string | null;
  try {
    installed = await get(INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY);
  } catch {
    installed = null;
  }
  return addonUnderstandsPaybacks(installed && installed.trim() ? installed : null) ? null : PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON;
}

/** Activity ids being filed right now: a second tap on the same row writes nothing. */
const inFlight = new Set<string>();
const errorText = (err: unknown) => (err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err));

/**
 * File one deposit as a payback: gate, re-read, plan, then the override, the
 * row, the category and the log, in that order. What a failure leaves:
 *  - the override not saved: nothing changed;
 *  - the row refused: read again — still a deposit, the override this run
 *    added comes back off ("nothing was changed"), or, when that write fails
 *    too, the next sync makes it a payback; can't tell, the override stays
 *    and says so; a payback after all (a lost response), carry on;
 *  - the category refused: a payback with no category, which /categorize
 *    offers again — logged, so Undo can still turn it back into a deposit;
 *  - the log not written: all done, without an Undo.
 * Wealthfolio's own error text goes to the host's log, never to the message.
 */
export async function filePayback(choice: PaybackChoice, io: PaybackIo, now: Date): Promise<PaybackOutcome> {
  if (inFlight.has(choice.activityId)) return { kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_RUNNING };
  inFlight.add(choice.activityId);
  try {
    return await fileOnce(choice, io, now);
  } finally {
    inFlight.delete(choice.activityId);
  }
}

async function fileOnce(choice: PaybackChoice, io: PaybackIo, now: Date): Promise<PaybackOutcome> {
  const refused = (message: string): PaybackOutcome => ({ kind: 'refused', message });
  const gate = await io.gate().catch(() => PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON);
  if (gate) return refused(gate);

  let row: PaybackRow | null;
  let plan: PaybackPlan;
  try {
    row = await io.read(choice.activityId);
    const feeds = row ? await io.feeds(row.accountId) : [];
    const overrides = await loadSyncOverrides(io.secrets.get);
    plan = planPayback({ row, feeds, overrides, category: { id: choice.categoryId, name: choice.categoryName } });
  } catch (err) {
    io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: ${errorText(err)}`);
    return refused(PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE);
  }
  if (!plan.ok) return refused(plan.reason);
  const w = plan.writes;
  const absCents = row!.absCents;

  // 1. The override, re-read right before it is written. Only what this run adds is ever taken back.
  let added: string[] = [];
  try {
    await editSyncOverrides(io.secrets.get, io.secrets.set, (o) => {
      added = w.txKeys.filter((k) => !hasTxOverride(o, k, 'asReimbursement'));
      return w.txKeys.reduce((d, k) => setTxOverride(d, k, 'asReimbursement', now, { label: w.label }), o);
    }, now);
  } catch (err) {
    io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: the override was not saved: ${errorText(err)}`);
    return refused(isUnreadableOverridesError(err) ? SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE : PAYBACK_OVERRIDE_FAILED);
  }

  // 2. The row. A refused update is read again: its response can be lost after it landed.
  try {
    await io.update(w.update);
  } catch (err) {
    io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: the update failed: ${errorText(err)}`);
    let again: PaybackRow | null;
    try {
      again = await io.read(choice.activityId);
    } catch (readErr) {
      io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: could not tell whether the update landed: ${errorText(readErr)}`);
      return { kind: 'partial', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_UNSURE };
    }
    if (!again || !isPaybackRow(again)) {
      try {
        if (added.length > 0) {
          await editSyncOverrides(io.secrets.get, io.secrets.set, (o) => added.reduce((d, k) => clearTxOverride(d, k, 'asReimbursement'), o), now);
        }
      } catch (undoErr) {
        io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: taking the override back failed: ${errorText(undoErr)}`);
        return { kind: 'partial', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_STUCK };
      }
      return refused(PAYBACK_UPDATE_REFUSED);
    }
  }

  // 3. The category.
  let filed = true;
  try {
    await io.assign(w.assign.activityId, w.assign.categoryId);
  } catch (err) {
    filed = false;
    io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: filing it under ${choice.categoryId} failed: ${errorText(err)}`);
  }

  // 4. The change log, which never costs the payback.
  const entry = filed ? w.entry : { ...w.entry, categoryId: null, categoryName: null };
  let logged = true;
  try {
    await io.record(paybackFiledChange(entry));
  } catch (err) {
    logged = false;
    io.log(`Payback ${choice.activityId}: filed, but the change log was not written: ${errorText(err)}: ${JSON.stringify(entry)}`);
  }
  const tail = logged ? PAYBACK_UNDO_HINT : PAYBACK_NO_UNDO;
  return filed
    ? { kind: 'filed', message: `${paybackDone(choice.categoryName, choice.parentName ?? choice.categoryName, absCents)}\n${tail}` }
    : { kind: 'partial', message: `${paybackUnfiled(choice.categoryName)}\n${tail}` };
}
```

---

### Task 4: Undo and Redo of a payback, and both gates

Q1d. `payback-filed` is undoable and companion-only. The runner re-reads the row by exact id through `UndoIo.paybacks.read` (with its instant) and its spending assignment, and the planner refuses a row that is gone, half of a linked transfer, or not where the last write left it (Undo: a `CREDIT`/`REIMBURSEMENT` filed under the entry's category; Redo: a `DEPOSIT` with no spending filing). Undo: `asReimbursement` cleared on every key, the row re-typed to `DEPOSIT` with its old subtype (`''` clears), the category taken off; Redo the reverse. When the re-type fails, every override this run touched goes back as it was ("nothing was changed"), or the message says it could not; when the category step fails after the re-type, the reversal is recorded and the message names the state. Telegram's Undo and Redo wait for an installed add-on of 1.62.0 (`PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON`); the app shows "Undo this from Telegram: /changes", or "Update the companion first" while the companion is older than 1.62.0.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/change-undo-payback.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/change-undo-run.ts`, `shared/change-undo.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (`PaybackRow`, `isPaybackRow`, `paybackRetype`, `paybackFiledChange`, the copy); Task 3 (`filePayback`, `paybackRowOf` — the round-trip test files through it); Task 1 (both gates).
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/change-undo.ts
COMPANION_ONLY_KINDS.has('payback-filed'); UNDO_KINDS.has('payback-filed');   // true
addonGateCovers({ kind: 'payback-filed' });                                    // true
// UndoState gains payback?: PaybackRow | null
// undo: clear-tx asReimbursement (each key) → update DEPOSIT (old subtype, '' clears) → unassign spending
// redo: set-tx asReimbursement (each key) → update CREDIT/REIMBURSEMENT → assign spending
// shared/change-undo-run.ts — UndoIo gains: paybacks?: { read(activityId: string): Promise<PaybackRow | null> }
```

```ts
import { runChangeUndo, type UndoIo } from './change-undo-run.js';
```
