```ts
import {
  PAYBACK_REDO_UNCHANGED, PAYBACK_UNDO_STUCK, PAYBACK_UNDO_UNCHANGED, PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON, paybackRedoHalf, paybackUndoHalf,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import type { HostActivity } from './sync-host.js';
import {
  emptySyncOverrides, hasTxOverride, parseSyncOverrides, setTxOverride, SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY, SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE,
} from './sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from './transfers.js';
import { INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY } from './version.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-1', 'TRN-77');
const SPENDING = 'spending_categories';

const DEPOSIT: HostActivity = {
  id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-a', activityType: 'DEPOSIT', date: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z', amount: 14,
  comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', sourceGroupId: null, subtype: null, metadata: null,
};

/** Wealthfolio over a fake host, the add-on's secrets, the spending assignments, and the change log. */
function harness(rows: HostActivity[] = [{ ...DEPOSIT }], secrets: Record<string, string> = {}) {
  const fh = createFakeHost({
    mapping: { 'sfin-1': 'wf-a' }, accountTypes: { 'wf-a': 'CASH' }, accountNames: { 'wf-a': 'Checking' }, existing: new Map([['wf-a', rows]]),
  });
  const data = new Map(Object.entries({ [INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY]: '1.62.0', ...secrets }));
  const filed = new Map<string, string>();
  const log: ChangeEntry[] = [];
  const secretsIo = {
    get: async (k: string) => data.get(k) ?? null,
    set: vi.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => { data.set(k, v); }),
  };
  const record = vi.fn(async (d: ChangeDraft) => { log.unshift(stampChange(d, 'companion', NOW)); });
  /** The row as Wealthfolio reports it now, the way a payback reads it. */
  const read = async (id: string) => {
    const r = [...fh.activities.values()].flat().find((x) => x.id === id);
    return r ? paybackRowOf({ ...r, currency: 'USD', accountName: 'Checking' }, 'CASH') : null;
  };
  const categories = {
    assignments: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (filed.has(id) ? [{ taxonomyId: SPENDING, categoryId: filed.get(id)! }] : [])),
    assign: vi.fn(async (id: string, _t: string, cat: string) => { filed.set(id, cat); }),
    unassign: vi.fn(async (id: string) => { filed.delete(id); }),
  };
  const io: UndoIo = {
    host: fh.host, secrets: secretsIo, categories, paybacks: { read }, record, where: { host: 'companion', companionVersion: '1.62.0' },
  };
  const paybackIo: PaybackIo = {
    gate: async () => null,
    read,
    feeds: async () => ['sfin-1'],
    secrets: secretsIo,
    update: async (w) => {
      const r = await fh.host.saveMany({ updates: [w] });
      if (r.errors.length) throw new Error(r.errors[0].message);
    },
    assign: async (id, cat) => { filed.set(id, cat); },
    record,
    log: () => {},
  };
  const asItem = (e: ChangeEntry): ChangeLogItem => {
    const latest = log.find((x) => x.reverses?.id === e.id);
    return latest?.reverses?.op === 'undo' ? { ...e, undone: { by: latest.id, at: latest.at } } : { ...e };
  };
  const undo = (e: ChangeEntry, op: UndoOp) => runChangeUndo(asItem(e), [...log], op, io, 'plain', NOW);
  const overrides = () => {
    const p = parseSyncOverrides(data.get(SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY));
    return p.kind === 'ok' ? p.overrides : emptySyncOverrides();
  };
  const file = async () => {
    await filePayback({ activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', parentName: 'Food & Dining' }, paybackIo, NOW);
    return log.find((e) => e.kind === 'payback-filed')!;
  };
  return { fh, data, filed, log, io, read, undo, overrides, file, set: secretsIo.set, record, categories };
}

describe('Undo and Redo of a payback', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] });
    vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('is an exact round trip: Undo puts the deposit back as Wealthfolio had it, Redo makes it the payback again', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const before = await h.read('act-7');
    const entry = await h.file();
    const payback = await h.read('act-7');
    expect(payback).toMatchObject({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', instant: '2026-10-09T12:00:00Z' });

    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toEqual({ ok: true, message: "Undone — it's a deposit again, off Restaurants.", needsSync: false });
    expect(await h.read('act-7')).toEqual(before);
    expect(h.filed.has('act-7')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasTxOverride(h.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(false);

    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'redo')).toEqual({ ok: true, message: "Redone — it's a payback under Restaurants again.", needsSync: false });
    expect(await h.read('act-7')).toEqual(payback);
    expect(h.filed.get('act-7')).toBe('cat-rest');
    expect(h.overrides().tx[KEY]).toMatchObject({ asReimbursement: true, label: 'VENMO CASHOUT · Checking' });

    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
    expect(await h.read('act-7')).toEqual(before);
    expect(h.log.filter((e) => e.reverses).map((e) => e.reverses!.op)).toEqual(['undo', 'redo', 'undo']);
  });

  it('puts back a subtype the deposit had before', async () => {
    const h = harness([{ ...DEPOSIT, subtype: 'BONUS' }]);
    const entry = await h.file();
    await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
    expect(await h.read('act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: 'BONUS' });
  });

  it('undoes a payback Wealthfolio never filed without touching categories', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const entry = stampChange(paybackFiledChange({
      account: { sfinId: 'sfin-1', wfId: 'wf-a', name: 'Checking' }, txKeys: [KEY], activityId: 'act-7', description: 'VENMO CASHOUT',
      amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', subtypeBefore: null, categoryId: null, categoryName: null,
    }), 'companion', NOW);
    h.fh.activities.set('wf-a', [{ ...DEPOSIT, activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' }]);
    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true, message: "Undone — it's a deposit again." });
    expect(h.categories.unassign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it.each<[string, (h: ReturnType<typeof harness>) => void, string]>([
    ['its category moved since', (h) => { h.filed.set('act-7', 'cat-gro'); }, 'It has changed since — nothing was undone.'],
    ['it is a deposit again', (h) => { h.fh.activities.set('wf-a', [{ ...DEPOSIT }]); }, 'It has changed since — nothing was undone.'],
    ['it is gone', (h) => { h.fh.activities.set('wf-a', []); }, 'That transaction is gone — nothing was undone.'],
    ['it is half of a linked transfer now', (h) => {
      h.fh.activities.set('wf-a', [{ ...DEPOSIT, activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', sourceGroupId: 'g1' }]);
    }, 'Undo the transfer link first.'],
  ])('refuses an Undo when %s, and writes nothing', async (_name, change, message) => {
    const h = harness();
    const entry = await h.file();
    change(h);
    h.set.mockClear();
    const saves = h.fh.saved.length;
    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message });
    expect(h.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.fh.saved.length).toBe(saves);
    expect(h.categories.unassign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('refuses a Redo of a deposit filed since, and writes nothing', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const entry = await h.file();
    await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
    h.filed.set('act-7', 'inc-other');
    h.set.mockClear();
    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'redo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: 'It has changed since — nothing was redone.' });
    expect(h.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it.each([['an add-on older than 1.62.0', '1.61.0'], ['an add-on the companion cannot see', '']])(
    'Telegram refuses Undo and Redo with %s, naming the app’s Change log, and writes nothing',
    async (_name, installed) => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      h.data.set(INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY, installed);
      h.set.mockClear();
      expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON });
      expect(await runChangeUndo({ ...entry, undone: { by: 'x', at: NOW.toISOString() } }, [], 'redo', h.io, 'plain', NOW))
        .toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON });
      expect(h.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    },
  );

  it('the app points at Telegram, once the companion knows paybacks — and waits for one that does', () => {
    const entry: ChangeLogItem = stampChange(paybackFiledChange({
      account: { sfinId: 'sfin-1', wfId: 'wf-a', name: 'Checking' }, txKeys: [KEY], activityId: 'act-7', description: 'VENMO CASHOUT',
      amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09', subtypeBefore: null, categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
    }), 'companion', NOW);
    const app = (companionVersion: string | null) => ({ host: 'addon' as const, companionVersion });
    expect(UNDO_KINDS.has('payback-filed') && COMPANION_ONLY_KINDS.has('payback-filed')).toBe(true);
    expect(undoAvailability(entry, app('1.62.0'))).toEqual({ kind: 'telegram', op: 'undo', text: UNDO_FROM_TELEGRAM });
    expect(UNDO_FROM_TELEGRAM).toBe('Undo this from Telegram: /changes');
    expect(undoAvailability({ ...entry, undone: { by: 'x', at: NOW.toISOString() } }, app('1.62.0')))
      .toEqual({ kind: 'telegram', op: 'redo', text: REDO_FROM_TELEGRAM });
    expect(undoAvailability(entry, app('1.61.0'))).toEqual({ kind: 'update-companion', op: 'undo', text: UNDO_UPDATE_COMPANION });
    expect(undoAvailability(entry, app(null))).toEqual({ kind: 'none' });
    expect(undoAvailability(entry, { host: 'companion', companionVersion: '1.61.0' })).toEqual({ kind: 'ready', op: 'undo' });
  });

  it('never overwrites an overrides document it cannot read', async () => {
    const h = harness();
    const entry = await h.file();
    h.data.set(SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY, '{oops');
    const saves = h.fh.saved.length;
    expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: SYNC_OVERRIDES_UNREADABLE_MESSAGE });
    expect(h.fh.saved.length).toBe(saves);
  });

  describe('a write that fails partway', () => {
    it('Undo: Wealthfolio refuses the re-type — the override goes back exactly as it was, and nothing changed', async () => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      const kept = h.overrides().tx[KEY];
      const payback = await h.read('act-7');
      h.fh.host.saveMany = async () => ({ created: [], updated: [], errors: [{ action: 'update', message: '400 - Invalid data' }] });
      const out = await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
      expect(out).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UNDO_UNCHANGED });
      expect(out.message).not.toContain('Invalid data');
      expect(h.overrides().tx[KEY]).toEqual(kept);
      expect(await h.read('act-7')).toEqual(payback);
      expect(h.filed.get('act-7')).toBe('cat-rest');
      expect(h.log.some((e) => e.reverses)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('Undo: says so when the override cannot be put back either', async () => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      h.fh.host.saveMany = async () => { throw new Error('ECONNRESET'); };
      let writes = 0;
      const set = h.io.secrets.set;
      h.io.secrets.set = async (k, v) => { writes += 1; if (writes > 1) throw new Error('500'); await set(k, v); };
      expect(await h.undo(entry, 'undo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UNDO_STUCK });
    });

    it('Undo: the category cannot come off — a deposit again, still filed, named, and recorded', async () => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      h.categories.unassign.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('500 Internal Server Error'));
      const out = await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
      expect(out).toEqual({ ok: false, message: paybackUndoHalf('Restaurants') });
      expect(out.message).toBe("Couldn't finish the undo — it's a deposit again, but it's still filed under Restaurants. Take that off in Wealthfolio.");
      expect(await h.read('act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: null });
      expect(h.filed.get('act-7')).toBe('cat-rest');
      expect(h.log[0].reverses).toEqual({ id: entry.id, op: 'undo' });
    });

    it('Redo: Wealthfolio refuses the re-type — the override it set comes back off', async () => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
      h.fh.host.saveMany = async () => ({ created: [], updated: [], errors: [{ action: 'update', message: '400' }] });
      expect(await h.undo(entry, 'redo')).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_REDO_UNCHANGED });
      expect(hasTxOverride(h.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(false);
      expect(await h.read('act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'DEPOSIT' });
    });

    it('Redo: the category is refused after the re-type — a payback with no category, named', async () => {
      const h = harness();
      const entry = await h.file();
      await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
      h.categories.assign.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('400 - Neutral transfers cannot be categorized.'));
      const out = await h.undo(entry, 'redo');
      expect(out).toEqual({ ok: false, message: paybackRedoHalf('Restaurants') });
      expect(out.message).not.toContain('Neutral');
      expect(await h.read('act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' });
    });

    it('keeps an override another undo set on the same row', async () => {
      const h = harness([{ ...DEPOSIT }], {
        [SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY]: JSON.stringify(setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), KEY, 'noVenmoNote', NOW)),
      });
      const entry = await h.file();
      await h.undo(entry, 'undo');
      expect(h.overrides().tx[KEY]).toMatchObject({ noVenmoNote: true });
      expect(hasTxOverride(h.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(false);
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
 * In Telegram, this waits for the INSTALLED add-on to read the override it
```


with:

```ts
      });
    }

    // A payback (1.62, W7): the override first, then the row re-typed, then the
    // category — Redo the same again. Each refuses a row that is not where the
    // last write left it.
    case 'payback-filed': {
      const keys = entry.subject.txKeys ?? [];
      if (keys.length === 0) return refuse(UNDO_NO_KEY);
      const row = state.payback ?? null;
      if (!row) return refuse(undoGone(op));
      if (row.grouped) return refuse(UNDO_LINKED_REFUSAL);
      const categoryId = str(after.categoryId);
      const categoryName = str(after.categoryName);
      const filedNow = (state.assignments ?? []).find((a) => a.taxonomyId === SPENDING_TAXONOMY)?.categoryId ?? null;
      if (op === 'undo') {
        if (!isPaybackRow(row) || filedNow !== categoryId) return refuse(undoChangedSince(op));
        return plan({
          op, needsSync: false, rowAfter: row.id, done: paybackUndone(categoryName),
          steps: [
            ...keys.map((txKey) => ({ do: 'clear-tx', txKey, flag: 'asReimbursement' } as const)),
            // The deposit's own subtype back; '' clears REIMBURSEMENT.
            { do: 'update', write: paybackRetype(row, 'DEPOSIT', str(before.subtype) ?? '') },
            ...(categoryId ? [{ do: 'unassign', activityId: row.id, taxonomyId: SPENDING_TAXONOMY } as const] : []),
          ],
        });
      }
      if (row.type !== 'DEPOSIT' || filedNow !== null) return refuse(undoChangedSince(op));
      return plan({
        op, needsSync: false, rowAfter: row.id, done: paybackRedone(categoryName),
        steps: [
          ...keys.map((txKey) => ({ do: 'set-tx', txKey, flag: 'asReimbursement', label: labelOf(entry) } as const)),
          { do: 'update', write: paybackRetype(row, 'CREDIT', 'REIMBURSEMENT') },
          ...(categoryId ? [{ do: 'assign', activityId: row.id, taxonomyId: SPENDING_TAXONOMY, categoryId } as const] : []),
        ],
      });
    }

    default:
      return refuse(UNDO_NOT_POSSIBLE);
  }
```

### Task 5: The sync keeps a payback (W7)

Q1b in `runSyncCore`, which both hosts run. In Phase A, a transaction whose key carries `asReimbursement` — while its bank amount is money in and its account is a cash account — is planned as `CREDIT` with subtype `REIMBURSEMENT`, whatever a keyword or a rule says, and sits out of pair detection (pairing would type it a transfer). `changed()` then finds nothing to do for a payback already stored so; a row whose re-type never landed is re-typed; a new import comes in as a payback. Money out, another account type, or an override pruned after its year, and the row is typed as usual.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/sync-core-payback.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/sync-core.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`asReimbursement`).
- Produces: no new names: `runSyncCore` obeys `asReimbursement` on both hosts.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

**Create `shared/sync-core-payback.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createFakeHost, type FakeHostSeed } from './fake-host.js';
import { runSyncCore, VALUATION_POLL } from './sync-core.js';
import type { HostActivity } from './sync-host.js';
import { emptySyncOverrides, pruneTxOverrides, setTxOverride, type SyncOverrides } from './sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from './transfers.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const epoch = (iso: string) => Date.parse(iso) / 1000;
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-1', 'tx-1');

const payback = (at: Date = NOW): SyncOverrides => setTxOverride(emptySyncOverrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement', at, { label: 'VENMO CASHOUT · Checking' });

/** Checking (a cash account), whose feed carries one Venmo cash-out of `amount`. */
const seed = (amount: string, extra: Partial<FakeHostSeed> = {}): FakeHostSeed => ({
  accountSet: { errors: [], accounts: [
    { id: 'sfin-1', name: 'Checking', currency: 'USD', balance: '0', 'balance-date': epoch('2026-10-14T12:00:00Z'),
      transactions: [{ id: 'tx-1', posted: epoch('2026-10-09T12:00:00Z'), amount, description: 'VENMO CASHOUT' }] },
  ] },
  mapping: { 'sfin-1': 'wf-a' },
  accountTypes: { 'wf-a': 'CASH' },
  accountNames: { 'wf-a': 'Checking' },
  ...extra,
});
const stored = (over: Partial<HostActivity> = {}): Map<string, HostActivity[]> => new Map([['wf-a', [{
  id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-a', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', date: '2026-10-09', amount: 14,
  comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · tx-1', sourceGroupId: null, ...over,
}]]]);
const row = (fh: ReturnType<typeof createFakeHost>) => fh.activities.get('wf-a')!.find((r) => r.comment?.endsWith('tx-1'))!;

describe('runSyncCore — a payback (asReimbursement)', () => {
  beforeEach(() => {
    VALUATION_POLL.delayMs = 1;
    VALUATION_POLL.attempts = 1;
    vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] });
    vi.setSystemTime(NOW);
  });
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('is never reverted: a payback the feed calls a deposit stays a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT, sync after sync', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', { existing: stored(), syncOverrides: payback() }));
    expect((await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {})).errors).toEqual([]);
    expect(fh.saved.filter((r) => (r.updates ?? []).length > 0 || (r.creates ?? []).length > 0)).toEqual([]);
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, { force: true });
    expect(fh.saved.filter((r) => (r.updates ?? []).length > 0)).toEqual([]);
    expect(row(fh)).toMatchObject({ activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' });
  });

  it('makes a row whose re-type never landed a payback', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', { existing: stored({ activityType: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: null }), syncOverrides: payback() }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh)).toMatchObject({ id: 'act-7', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' });
  });

  it('imports a new row as a payback when the override came first', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', { syncOverrides: payback() }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh)).toMatchObject({ activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', amount: 14 });
  });

  it('beats a keyword that would type it a transfer, and is never paired or linked', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', {
      accountSet: { errors: [], accounts: [
        { id: 'sfin-1', name: 'Checking', currency: 'USD', balance: '0', 'balance-date': epoch('2026-10-14T12:00:00Z'),
          transactions: [{ id: 'tx-1', posted: epoch('2026-10-09T12:00:00Z'), amount: '14.00', description: 'ONLINE TRANSFER FROM SAV' }] },
        { id: 'sfin-2', name: 'Savings', currency: 'USD', balance: '0', 'balance-date': epoch('2026-10-14T12:00:00Z'),
          transactions: [{ id: 'tx-2', posted: epoch('2026-10-09T12:00:00Z'), amount: '-14.00', description: 'ONLINE TRANSFER TO CHK' }] },
      ] },
      mapping: { 'sfin-1': 'wf-a', 'sfin-2': 'wf-b' },
      accountTypes: { 'wf-a': 'CASH', 'wf-b': 'CASH' },
      syncOverrides: payback(),
    }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh)).toMatchObject({ activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', sourceGroupId: null });
    expect(fh.links).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('yields when the bank turns it into money out: typed as usual', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('-14.00', { existing: stored(), syncOverrides: payback() }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh).activityType).toBe('WITHDRAWAL');
  });

  it('yields on an account that is not cash', async () => {
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', { accountTypes: { 'wf-a': 'CREDIT_CARD' }, syncOverrides: payback() }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh)).toMatchObject({ activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: null });
  });

  it('yields once its year runs out and the next overrides write prunes it', async () => {
    const lapsed = pruneTxOverrides(payback(new Date('2025-10-13T15:00:00Z')), NOW);
    expect(lapsed.tx).toEqual({});
    const fh = createFakeHost(seed('14.00', { existing: stored(), syncOverrides: lapsed }));
    await runSyncCore(fh.host, fh.store, {});
    expect(row(fh).activityType).toBe('DEPOSIT');
  });
});
```

### Task 6: The menu: It's a payback », the payback grid, the outcome screens

W6 in the pure menu. The switch beside a deposit's income grid reads **It's a payback »**; the grid it opens — or the only grid, when there are no income categories — files a **payback** for every spending category (`{ kind: 'payback', activityId, categoryId }`), one level down too (`subcats.payback`, whose Back returns to the payback grid), and says what it is for ("Pick the spending it pays you back for."). The one-tap guess is never shown there. Money out, a card credit and `/recategorize` file exactly as before. Two screens show the runner's words, escaped whole: `paybackResult` (Next transaction, Done; `offerRules` is read by Task 9) and `paybackRefused` (Back to the payback grid, Done). Three existing tests of the old switch are updated.

- Create: `shared/categorize-menu-payback.test.ts`
- Modify: `shared/categorize-menu.test.ts`, `shared/categorize-menu.ts`

**Create `shared/categorize-menu-payback.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import {
  applyTap, renderScreen, type CategorizeTxn, type MenuAction, type MenuScreen, type MenuSession, type SpendingCategory,
} from './categorize-menu.js';
import { PAYBACK_PICK, PAYBACK_SWITCH } from './payback-copy.js';

const FOOD: SpendingCategory = { id: 'cat-food', name: 'Food & Dining', parentId: null, parentName: null };
const REST: SpendingCategory = { id: 'cat-rest', name: 'Restaurants', parentId: 'cat-food', parentName: 'Food & Dining' };
const FUN: SpendingCategory = { id: 'cat-fun', name: 'Entertainment', parentId: null, parentName: null };
const SALARY: SpendingCategory = { id: 'inc-sal', name: 'Salary', parentId: null, parentName: null };

const deposit = (over: Partial<CategorizeTxn> = {}): CategorizeTxn => ({
  activityId: 'act-7', date: '2026-10-09', amountCents: 1400, description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', accountName: 'PNC Checking',
  accountId: 'wf-chk', direction: 'in', incomeEligible: true, ...over,
});
const session = (screen: MenuScreen, over: Partial<MenuSession> = {}): MenuSession => ({
  txns: [deposit()], categories: [FOOD, REST, FUN], incomeCategories: [SALARY], screen, buttons: [], generation: 7, mode: 'categorize', ...over,
});
const view = (s: MenuSession) => {
  const r = renderScreen(s);
  const labels = r.keyboard.inline_keyboard.flat().map((b) => b.text);
  const action = (label: string): MenuAction => r.buttons[labels.indexOf(label)];
  return { ...r, labels, action };
};
const maxBytes = (s: MenuSession) => Math.max(...renderScreen(s).keyboard.inline_keyboard.flat()
  .map((b) => new TextEncoder().encode(b.callback_data).length));

describe('the payback grid (W6)', () => {
  it('offers "It’s a payback »" beside a deposit’s income grid', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' }));
    expect(PAYBACK_SWITCH).toBe("It's a payback »");
    expect(v.labels).toContain(PAYBACK_SWITCH);
    expect(v.labels).not.toContain('Spending categories »');
    expect(v.action(PAYBACK_SWITCH)).toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true } });
  });

  it('files every spending category there as a payback, and says what the grid is for', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true }));
    expect(v.text).toBe(`VENMO CASHOUT\n+$14 · Oct 9 · PNC Checking\n${PAYBACK_PICK}`);
    expect(v.action('Entertainment')).toEqual({ kind: 'payback', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' });
    expect(v.action('Food & Dining')).toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'subcats', activityId: 'act-7', parentId: 'cat-food', payback: true } });
    expect(v.action('« Income categories')).toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' } });
  });

  it('keeps a payback a payback one level down, and Back returns to the payback grid', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'subcats', activityId: 'act-7', parentId: 'cat-food', payback: true }));
    expect(v.action('Restaurants')).toEqual({ kind: 'payback', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' });
    expect(v.action('Just Food & Dining itself')).toEqual({ kind: 'payback', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-food' });
    expect(v.action('‹ Back')).toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true } });
  });

  it('gives a deposit the payback grid straight away when there is no income grid', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' }, { incomeCategories: [] }));
    expect(v.labels).not.toContain(PAYBACK_SWITCH);
    expect(v.action('Entertainment')).toEqual({ kind: 'payback', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' });
  });

  it('never offers the one-tap guess there: every tap goes through the payback', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true }, {
      txns: [deposit({ guess: { categoryId: 'cat-fun', name: 'Entertainment', count: 3 } })],
    }));
    expect(v.labels.some((l) => l.startsWith('✓'))).toBe(false);
  });

  it('leaves money out, a card credit and /recategorize filing as before', () => {
    const out = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' }, { txns: [deposit({ direction: 'out', incomeEligible: false })] }));
    expect(out.action('Entertainment')).toEqual({ kind: 'assign', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' });
    expect(out.text).not.toContain(PAYBACK_PICK);
    const card = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' }, { txns: [deposit({ incomeEligible: false })] }));
    expect(card.action('Entertainment')).toEqual({ kind: 'assign', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' });
    const recat = view(session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7' }, {
      mode: 'recategorize', txns: [deposit({ currentCategory: { taxonomyId: 'income_sources', categoryId: 'inc-sal', name: 'Salary' } })],
    }));
    expect(recat.action('Entertainment')).toEqual({ kind: 'reassign', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' });
  });

  it('resolves a tap on a payback button', () => {
    const s = session({ kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true });
    const r = renderScreen(s);
    s.buttons = r.buttons;
    const fun = r.keyboard.inline_keyboard.flat().find((b) => b.text === 'Entertainment')!;
    expect(applyTap(s, fun.callback_data)).toEqual({ ok: true, action: { kind: 'payback', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-fun' } });
  });
});

describe('the payback’s outcome', () => {
  it('shows the runner’s words, escaped, then the way on', () => {
    const v = view(session({
      kind: 'paybackResult', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', offerRules: false,
      text: 'Filed as a payback under Dine_Out — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.',
    }));
    expect(v.text).toBe('Filed as a payback under Dine\\_Out — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.');
    expect(v.labels).toEqual(['Next transaction', 'Done']);
  });

  it('shows a refusal with Back to the payback grid', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'paybackRefused', activityId: 'act-7', text: "It's still pending — file it as a payback once it posts." }));
    expect(v.text).toBe("It's still pending — file it as a payback once it posts.");
    expect(v.action('‹ Back')).toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true } });
  });

  it('keeps every button within 64 bytes, at any generation', () => {
    for (const screen of [
      { kind: 'txn', activityId: 'act-7', showSpending: true },
      { kind: 'subcats', activityId: 'act-7', parentId: 'cat-food', payback: true },
      { kind: 'paybackResult', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', offerRules: true, text: 'x' },
      { kind: 'paybackRefused', activityId: 'act-7', text: 'x' },
    ] as MenuScreen[]) {
      expect(maxBytes(session(screen, { generation: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER }))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(64);
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
function renderSubcats(session: MenuSession, activityId: string, parentId: string): RenderResult {
  const txn = findTxn(session, activityId);
  const parent = findCategory(session, parentId);
  if (!txn || !parent) return renderList(session, 0, GONE_NOTE);

  const children = session.categories.filter((c) => c.parentId === parentId);
  const childButtons: Btn[] = children.map((c) => ({
    text: c.name,
    action: assignAction(session.mode, activityId, c.id),
  }));

  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    ...childButtons.map((b) => [b]),
    [{ text: `Just ${parent.name} itself`, action: assignAction(session.mode, activityId, parent.id) }],
    [{ text: '‹ Back', action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId } } }],
  ];

  const text = `Choose a subcategory of ${escapeMarkdown(parent.name)}:`;
```

```ts
function renderSubcats(session: MenuSession, activityId: string, parentId: string, payback = false): RenderResult {
  const txn = findTxn(session, activityId);
  const parent = findCategory(session, parentId);
  if (!txn || !parent) return renderList(session, 0, GONE_NOTE);

  // From the payback grid every tap files a payback, and Back returns there.
  const pick = (categoryId: string): MenuAction => (payback
    ? { kind: 'payback', activityId, categoryId }
    : assignAction(session.mode, activityId, categoryId));
  const children = session.categories.filter((c) => c.parentId === parentId);
  const childButtons: Btn[] = children.map((c) => ({
    text: c.name,
    action: pick(c.id),
  }));

  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    ...childButtons.map((b) => [b]),
    [{ text: `Just ${parent.name} itself`, action: pick(parent.id) }],
    [{ text: '‹ Back', action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId, ...(payback ? { showSpending: true } : {}) } } }],
  ];

  const text = `Choose a subcategory of ${escapeMarkdown(parent.name)}:`;
```

```ts
/** A payback's outcome: the runner's words, escaped — a bank or category name
 *  can carry `_` or `*` — and the way on. */
function renderPaybackResult(session: MenuSession, screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'paybackResult' }>): RenderResult {
  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    [{ text: 'Next transaction', action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'list', page: 0 } } }],
    [DONE_BTN],
  ];
  return finish(session.generation, escapeMarkdown(screen.text), rows);
}

/** A payback refused: why, escaped, and Back to the payback grid. */
function renderPaybackRefused(session: MenuSession, screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'paybackRefused' }>): RenderResult {
  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    [{ text: '‹ Back', action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'txn', activityId: screen.activityId, showSpending: true } } }],
    [DONE_BTN],
  ];
  return finish(session.generation, escapeMarkdown(screen.text), rows);
}

function renderClosed(generation: number): RenderResult {
  return finish(generation, 'Menu closed.', []);
}
```

### Task 7: Plain words for a refused category (W9)

Every `assignActivityCategory` failure in the controller — a first filing, a move's new category, a move's Undo — now says what did not happen in the add-on's words and never forwards Wealthfolio's text, which goes to the log. `assignRefusalOf` reads Wealthfolio's own refusals by their words (the bucket check and the spending-scope check, the same sentences on 3.8–3.10) into `neutral`, `wrong-bucket`, `scope` or `off`, and `assignFailureText` adds the matching sentence — the same ones the refusal screens use, without the hint. An unassign that fails keeps its present words. Four existing tests change their expected text.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/assign-refusal.test.ts`
- Modify: `companion/src/categorize.test.ts`, `companion/src/categorize.ts`, `shared/cash-flow-bucket.ts`, `shared/categorize-menu.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/cash-flow-bucket.ts
export type AssignRefusal = 'neutral' | 'wrong-bucket' | 'scope' | 'off';
export function assignRefusalOf(message: string): AssignRefusal | null;
// shared/categorize-menu.ts
export const ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT: string;        // "Couldn't file that — Wealthfolio didn't take it, so it still has no category."
export const REFILE_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT: string; // "Couldn't move that — Wealthfolio didn't take the new category."
export const UNDO_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT: string;   // "Couldn't undo that — Wealthfolio didn't take the old category back."
export function assignFailureText(lead: string, message: string): string;
```

**Create `shared/assign-refusal.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { assignRefusalOf } from './cash-flow-bucket.js';
import { assignFailureText, ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, REFILE_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, UNDO_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT } from './categorize-menu.js';

describe('a refused assignment (W9)', () => {
  it.each([
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Neutral transfers cannot be categorized. Change or unlink the transfer if it should count as spending.', 'neutral'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Income activities can only use income categories. Categories label the cash-flow bucket; they do not change it.', 'wrong-bucket'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Spending activities can only use spending categories. Categories label the cash-flow bucket; they do not change it.', 'wrong-bucket'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Activity account is not opted into spending tracking', 'scope'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Activity account does not support spending tracking', 'scope'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Spending tracking is disabled', 'off'],
    ['assignActivityCategory failed: 403 - Forbidden', null],
    ['fetch failed', null],
  ])('reads "%s" as %s', (message, reason) => {
    expect(assignRefusalOf(message)).toBe(reason);
  });

  it('leads with what did not happen and adds the reason in its own words — never Wealthfolio’s', () => {
    const raw = 'assignActivityCategory failed: 400 - Income activities can only use income categories. Categories label the cash-flow bucket; they do not change it.';
    expect(assignFailureText(ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, raw)).toBe(
      "Couldn't file that — Wealthfolio didn't take it, so it still has no category.\nIt is recorded as money in and can only take an income category while that is true.",
    );
    expect(assignFailureText(REFILE_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, 'socket hang up')).toBe("Couldn't move that — Wealthfolio didn't take the new category.");
    expect(assignFailureText(UNDO_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, '500')).toBe("Couldn't undo that — Wealthfolio didn't take the old category back.");
    for (const lead of [ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, REFILE_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT, UNDO_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT]) {
      expect(assignFailureText(lead, raw)).not.toMatch(/Income activities|Categories label/);
    }
  });
});
```

```ts
export function refundSubtypeWouldMakeSpending(input: BucketInput): boolean {
  return bucketFor({ ...input, subtype: REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE }) === 'spending';
}

/** Why Wealthfolio refused a category assignment, when it said why. */
export type AssignRefusal = 'neutral' | 'wrong-bucket' | 'scope' | 'off';

/**
 * Wealthfolio's own refusals of an assignment, read by their words (W9):
 * `ensure_activity_assignment_allowed` and `ensure_activity_in_spending_scope`
 * (crates/spending/src/cash_activities/service.rs:957-1018 and 1063-1098 at
 * 3.10.0, 901-962 and 1007-1042 at 3.8.0; the same sentences on 3.8, 3.9 and
 * 3.10). The caller maps the token to the add-on's own sentence and never
 * forwards Wealthfolio's text, which names internal labels ("Income
 * activities can only use income categories…") or misdescribes a merely
 * neutral row as a transfer. Null for anything else (a 403, a timeout).
 */
export function assignRefusalOf(message: string): AssignRefusal | null {
  if (/Neutral transfers cannot be categorized/i.test(message)) return 'neutral';
  if (/activities can only use \w+ categories/i.test(message)) return 'wrong-bucket';
  if (/not opted into spending tracking|does not support spending tracking/i.test(message)) return 'scope';
  if (/Spending tracking is disabled/i.test(message)) return 'off';
  return null;
}
```

```ts
/** The plain words for a refusal Wealthfolio gave when an assignment failed
 *  (W9) — the same sentences as the refusals above, without the hint. */
const ASSIGN_REFUSAL_LINES: Record<AssignRefusal, string> = {
  neutral: REFUSED_TEXT.neutral,
  'wrong-bucket': 'It is recorded as money in and can only take an income category while that is true.',
  scope: REFUSED_TEXT.scope,
  off: 'Spending tracking is off in Wealthfolio.',
};

/** A first filing Wealthfolio refused. */
export const ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT = "Couldn't file that — Wealthfolio didn't take it, so it still has no category.";
/** A recategorize whose new category Wealthfolio refused (the controller adds what stands). */
export const REFILE_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT = "Couldn't move that — Wealthfolio didn't take the new category.";
/** A recategorize Undo whose old category Wealthfolio refused (the controller adds what stands). */
export const UNDO_ASSIGN_FAILED_TEXT = "Couldn't undo that — Wealthfolio didn't take the old category back.";

/**
 * What a refused assignment says (W9): `lead`, then why in the add-on's own
 * words when Wealthfolio gave a reason it knows. Never Wealthfolio's text —
 * the caller logs that.
 */
export function assignFailureText(lead: string, message: string): string {
  const reason = assignRefusalOf(message);
  return reason ? `${lead}\n${ASSIGN_REFUSAL_LINES[reason]}` : lead;
}

function renderRefused(
  session: MenuSession,
  screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'refused' }>,
```

### Task 8: The payback in Telegram, end to end

W6 on the companion. `companion/src/payback-host.ts` lends `filePayback` the REST reads and writes: the row by exact id with its account's type and stored instant (`readPaybackRow`), the feeds from `account_mapping`, the add-on's secrets, one bulk update, the spending `PUT`, the companion's change-log writer, and the installed-add-on gate. The controller's `payback` tap re-reads the sweep (a row filed elsewhere meanwhile is gone, and nothing is written), runs `deps.payback` with the category and its parent, and shows `paybackResult` (republishing the tile) or `paybackRefused` — drawn from the sweep in hand, so a failed re-read cannot hide what was written. `index.ts` runs the payback like a fix (refused while the companion's own sync runs), runs a payback's `/changes` Undo the same way, and `companionUndoIo` reads a payback's row.

**Files:**
- Create: `companion/src/categorize-payback.test.ts`, `companion/src/payback-host.test.ts`, `companion/src/payback-host.ts`
- Modify: `companion/src/categorize.ts`, `companion/src/change-undo-host.ts`, `companion/src/index.test.ts`, `companion/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 (`filePayback`, `addonPaybackGate`, `PaybackIo`, `PaybackChoice`, `PaybackOutcome`); Task 2 (`paybackRowOf`, `PaybackRow`, the copy); Task 4 (`UndoIo.paybacks`); Task 6 (the screens and the `payback` action); `runCompanionFixExclusive`, `FIX_SYNC_RUNNING` (companion/src/index.ts).
- Produces:

```ts
// companion/src/payback-host.ts
export type PaybackClient = SecretClient & Pick<WealthfolioClient, 'searchActivities' | 'getAccounts' | 'saveMany' | 'assignActivityCategory'>;
export async function readPaybackRow(client: Pick<WealthfolioClient, 'searchActivities' | 'getAccounts'>, activityId: string): Promise<PaybackRow | null>;
export function companionPaybackIo(client: PaybackClient, log: (msg: string) => void): PaybackIo;
// companion/src/categorize.ts — CategorizeDeps gains payback?: (choice: PaybackChoice) => Promise<PaybackOutcome>
// companion/src/change-undo-host.ts — UndoClient gains 'searchActivities' | 'getAccounts'; companionUndoIo gives paybacks.read
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { InlineKeyboard } from '../../shared/telegram.js';
import type { PaybackChoice, PaybackOutcome } from '../../shared/payback.js';
import { PAYBACK_PICK, PAYBACK_SWITCH, PAYBACK_UNAVAILABLE, PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE } from '../../shared/payback-copy.js';
import { createCategorizeController, type CategorizeDeps } from './categorize.js';

const CATS = [
  { id: 'cat-food', name: 'Food & Dining', parentId: null, parentName: null },
  { id: 'cat-rest', name: 'Restaurants', parentId: 'cat-food', parentName: 'Food & Dining' },
  { id: 'cat-fun', name: 'Entertainment', parentId: null, parentName: null },
];
const DEPOSIT = {
  activityId: 'act-7', wfAccountId: 'wf-chk', notes: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', amountCents: 1400, date: '2026-10-09',
  accountName: 'PNC Checking', direction: 'in' as const, incomeEligible: true,
};
const FILED: PaybackOutcome = {
  kind: 'filed', message: 'Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.',
};

function setup(payback?: (c: PaybackChoice) => Promise<PaybackOutcome>) {
  let rows = [{ ...DEPOSIT }];
  const deps: CategorizeDeps = {
    dbPath: () => '/db',
    readRows: vi.fn(() => rows.map((r) => ({ ...r }))),
    readCategorized: vi.fn(() => []),
    readCategories: vi.fn(() => CATS.map((c) => ({ ...c }))),
    readIncomeCategories: vi.fn(() => [{ id: 'inc-oth', name: 'Other Income', parentId: null, parentName: null }]),
    readLedger: vi.fn(async () => ({})),
    writeLedgerMerged: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    assign: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    unassign: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    unassignTaxonomy: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    createRule: vi.fn(async () => null),
    republish: vi.fn(async () => {}),
    log: vi.fn(),
    ...(payback ? { payback: vi.fn(payback) } : {}),
  };
  const ui = { edit: vi.fn(async (_t: string, _k?: InlineKeyboard) => {}), answer: vi.fn(async () => {}), send: vi.fn(async () => {}) };
  const controller = createCategorizeController(deps);
  const tap = (data: string) => controller.onCallback({ data, chatId: 1, messageId: 2 }, ui);
  const last = () => ui.edit.mock.calls.at(-1) as unknown as [string, InlineKeyboard | undefined];
  const labels = (k?: InlineKeyboard) => (k?.inline_keyboard ?? []).flat().map((b) => b.text);
  const press = async (label: string) => {
    const btn = last()[1]!.inline_keyboard.flat().find((b) => b.text === label);
    if (!btn) throw new Error(`no "${label}" among ${labels(last()[1]).join(' | ')}`);
    await tap(btn.callback_data);
  };
  /** /categorize → the deposit → It's a payback » */
  const toPaybackGrid = async () => {
    const send = vi.fn(async (_t: string, _k?: InlineKeyboard) => {});
    await controller.open(send);
    const k = send.mock.calls[0][1]!;
    await tap(k.inline_keyboard.flat().find((b) => b.text.includes('VENMO CASHOUT'))!.callback_data);
    await press(PAYBACK_SWITCH);
  };
  return { deps, ui, tap, last, labels, press, toPaybackGrid, setRows: (r: typeof rows) => { rows = r; } };
}

describe('/categorize: a deposit filed as a payback (W6)', () => {
  it('runs the payback with the category and its parent, and shows what it says, escaped, with the way on', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => ({ ...FILED, message: FILED.message.replace('Restaurants', 'Dine_Out') }));
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(`VENMO CASHOUT\n+$14 · Oct 9 · PNC Checking\n${PAYBACK_PICK}`);
    await h.press('Food & Dining');
    await h.press('Restaurants');
    expect(h.deps.payback).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', parentName: 'Food & Dining' });
    expect(h.deps.assign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe('Filed as a payback under Dine\\_Out — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.');
    expect(h.labels(h.last()[1])).toEqual(['Next transaction', 'Done']);
    expect(h.deps.republish).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('shows a refusal with Back to the payback grid, and republishes nothing', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => ({ kind: 'refused', message: "It's still pending — file it as a payback once it posts." }));
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe("It's still pending — file it as a payback once it posts.");
    expect(h.deps.republish).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await h.press('‹ Back');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toContain(PAYBACK_PICK);
  });

  it('shows a partial payback as it stands', async () => {
    const message = "It's a payback now, but Wealthfolio didn't file it under Entertainment — /categorize offers it again.\nUndo it from /changes.";
    const h = setup(async () => ({ kind: 'partial', message }));
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(message);
  });

  it('writes nothing for a row filed elsewhere since the grid was drawn', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => FILED);
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    h.setRows([]);
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    expect(h.deps.payback).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(h.last()[0]).toContain('That transaction is no longer uncategorized.');
  });

  it('says it couldn’t reach Wealthfolio when the payback throws', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => { throw new Error('boom'); });
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE);
    expect(h.deps.log).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.stringContaining('boom'));
  });

  it('refuses in words when no payback is wired', async () => {
    const h = setup();
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(PAYBACK_UNAVAILABLE);
    expect(h.deps.assign).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
```

**Create `companion/src/payback-host.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import type { ChangeBucket, ChangeLogItem } from '../../shared/change-log.js';
import { runChangeUndo } from '../../shared/change-undo-run.js';
import { filePayback, type PaybackChoice } from '../../shared/payback.js';
import { PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON } from '../../shared/payback-copy.js';
import { hasTxOverride, parseSyncOverrides, SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY } from '../../shared/sync-overrides.js';
import { accountTxKey } from '../../shared/transfers.js';
import { INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY } from '../../shared/version.js';
import { companionUndoIo } from './change-undo-host.js';
import { companionPaybackIo, readPaybackRow, type PaybackClient } from './payback-host.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const KEY = accountTxKey('sfin-chk', 'TRN-77');
const RESTAURANTS: PaybackChoice = { activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants', parentName: 'Food & Dining' };

type Row = Record<string, unknown> & { id: string };
const DEPOSIT: Row = {
  id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', accountName: 'PNC Checking', activityType: 'DEPOSIT', date: '2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00',
  amount: '14', fee: '0', currency: 'USD', comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: null, sourceGroupId: null, metadata: null,
};

/** Wealthfolio over REST, in memory: exact-id search, accounts, the bulk endpoint, assignments, the add-on's secrets. */
function fakeClient(installed: string | null = '1.62.0') {
  const rows: Row[] = [{ ...DEPOSIT }];
  const data = new Map<string, string>([['account_mapping', JSON.stringify({ 'sfin-chk': 'wf-chk' })]]);
  if (installed !== null) data.set(INSTALLED_ADDON_VERSION_SECRET_KEY, installed);
  const filed = new Map<string, string>();
  const client = {
    getAddonSecret: vi.fn(async (_a: string, k: string) => data.get(k) ?? null),
    setAddonSecret: vi.fn(async (_a: string, k: string, v: string) => { data.set(k, v); }),
    getAccounts: vi.fn(async () => [{ id: 'wf-chk', accountType: 'CASH' }]),
    searchActivities: vi.fn(async (b: { activityIdFilter?: string[] }) => rows.filter((r) => b.activityIdFilter?.includes(r.id)) as any),
    saveMany: vi.fn(async (req: { updates?: Array<Record<string, any>> }) => {
      for (const w of req.updates ?? []) {
        const r = rows.find((x) => x.id === w.id)!;
        // Wealthfolio's patch: an explicit empty subtype stores none.
        Object.assign(r, { activityType: w.activityType, date: w.activityDate, subtype: w.subtype ? w.subtype : null, amount: String(w.amount) });
      }
      return { created: [], updated: [], errors: [] };
    }),
    assignActivityCategory: vi.fn(async (id: string, _t: string, cat: string) => { filed.set(id, cat); }),
    unassignActivityCategory: vi.fn(async (id: string) => { filed.delete(id); }),
    getActivityAssignments: vi.fn(async (id: string) => (filed.has(id) ? [{ taxonomyId: 'spending_categories', categoryId: filed.get(id)! }] : [])),
  };
  const logged = (): ChangeLogItem[] => [...data].filter(([k, v]) => k.startsWith('change_log:companion:') && v)
    .flatMap(([, v]) => (JSON.parse(v) as ChangeBucket).entries);
  const overrides = () => {
    const p = parseSyncOverrides(data.get(SYNC_OVERRIDES_SECRET_KEY));
    if (p.kind !== 'ok') throw new Error(p.kind);
    return p.overrides;
  };
  return { client: client as unknown as PaybackClient & typeof client, rows, data, filed, logged, overrides };
}

describe('a payback on the companion', () => {
  it('re-reads the row live, re-types it at its stored instant, files it and logs it in its own bucket', async () => {
    const f = fakeClient();
    const out = await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, companionPaybackIo(f.client, vi.fn()), NOW);
    expect(out.kind).toBe('filed');
    expect(f.client.searchActivities).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ page: 0, pageSize: 1, activityIdFilter: ['act-7'] });
    expect(f.client.saveMany).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ updates: [{
      id: 'act-7', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT', activityDate: '2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00', symbol: { symbol: '$CASH-USD' },
      amount: 14, currency: 'USD', comment: 'VENMO CASHOUT · TRN-77', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT',
    }] });
    expect(f.client.assignActivityCategory).toHaveBeenCalledWith('act-7', 'spending_categories', 'cat-rest');
    expect(hasTxOverride(f.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
    expect(f.logged()).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ writer: 'companion', actor: 'you', kind: 'payback-filed' })]);
  });

  it('refuses, and touches nothing, while the installed add-on is unknown', async () => {
    const f = fakeClient(null);
    expect(await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, companionPaybackIo(f.client, vi.fn()), NOW)).toEqual({ kind: 'refused', message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON });
    expect(f.client.searchActivities).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(f.client.saveMany).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('is undone and redone from /changes through the companion’s Undo host', async () => {
    const f = fakeClient();
    await filePayback(RESTAURANTS, companionPaybackIo(f.client, vi.fn()), NOW);
    const entry = f.logged()[0];
    const io = companionUndoIo(f.client as any, '/nope/wealthfolio.db');
    expect(await runChangeUndo(entry, [entry], 'undo', io, 'plain', NOW)).toMatchObject({ ok: true, message: "Undone — it's a deposit again, off Restaurants." });
    expect(await readPaybackRow(f.client, 'act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'DEPOSIT', subtype: null, instant: '2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00' });
    expect(f.filed.has('act-7')).toBe(false);
    expect(hasTxOverride(f.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(false);

    const undo = f.logged().find((e) => e.reverses?.op === 'undo')!;
    expect(await runChangeUndo({ ...entry, undone: { by: undo.id, at: undo.at } }, f.logged(), 'redo', io, 'plain', NOW))
      .toMatchObject({ ok: true, message: "Redone — it's a payback under Restaurants again." });
    expect(await readPaybackRow(f.client, 'act-7')).toMatchObject({ type: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', instant: '2026-10-09T00:00:00+00:00' });
    expect(f.filed.get('act-7')).toBe('cat-rest');
    expect(hasTxOverride(f.overrides(), KEY, 'asReimbursement')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

```ts
    read: (now) => readCompanionChangeLog(wfClient, now),
    undo: (entry, entries, op, variant) => {
      const run = () => runChangeUndo(entry, entries, op, companionUndoIo(wfClient, wealthfolioDbPath()), variant);
      // A removal's Undo/Redo writes the row and the skip list the sync reads,
      // a payback's the row and the overrides: refused while the companion's
      // own sync runs, and a sync waits for it.
      return entry.kind === 'transaction-removed' || entry.kind === 'payback-filed'
        ? runCompanionFixExclusive(run, (): UndoOutcome => ({ ok: false, message: FIX_SYNC_RUNNING }))
        : run();
    },
```

**Create `companion/src/payback-host.ts`:**

```ts
/**
 * companion/src/payback-host.ts
 *
 * The companion's half of a payback (1.62.0, W6–W8): what `filePayback`
 * (shared/payback.ts) and a payback's Undo read and write, bound to one
 * Wealthfolio client. The row is re-read live over REST by exact id
 * (`activityIdFilter`, Wealthfolio 3.8), with its account's type and its
 * stored instant; the re-type goes through the bulk endpoint, the category
 * through `PUT …/assignments`, and the entry through the companion's one
 * change-log writer. Telegram's paybacks wait for an INSTALLED add-on that
 * reads `asReimbursement` (W8).
 */
import { SPENDING_TAXONOMY_ID } from '../../shared/cash-flow-bucket.js';
import { addonPaybackGate, paybackRowOf, type PaybackIo } from '../../shared/payback.js';
import type { PaybackRow } from '../../shared/payback-row.js';
import { sfinIdsFor } from '../../shared/tx-story.js';
import { recordCompanionChanges, type SecretClient } from './change-log-host.js';
import type { WealthfolioClient } from './wealthfolio.js';

const ADDON_ID = 'simplefin-sync';

/** What a payback needs of a `WealthfolioClient`. */
export type PaybackClient = SecretClient & Pick<WealthfolioClient, 'searchActivities' | 'getAccounts' | 'saveMany' | 'assignActivityCategory'>;

/** One row by exact id, as a payback reads it; null when it is gone. Rejects when unreachable. */
export async function readPaybackRow(client: Pick<WealthfolioClient, 'searchActivities' | 'getAccounts'>, activityId: string): Promise<PaybackRow | null> {
  const types = new Map((await client.getAccounts()).map((a) => [a.id, String(a.accountType ?? '')]));
  const items = await client.searchActivities({ page: 0, pageSize: 1, activityIdFilter: [activityId] });
  const hit = items.find((a) => a.id === activityId);
  return hit ? paybackRowOf(hit, types.get(hit.accountId) ?? '') : null;
}

export function companionPaybackIo(client: PaybackClient, log: (msg: string) => void): PaybackIo {
  const get = (k: string) => client.getAddonSecret(ADDON_ID, k);
  return {
    gate: () => addonPaybackGate(get),
    read: (activityId) => readPaybackRow(client, activityId),
    feeds: async (wfAccountId) => {
      const raw = await get('account_mapping');
      return sfinIdsFor(raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as Record<string, string>) : {}, wfAccountId);
    },
    secrets: { get, set: (k, v) => client.setAddonSecret(ADDON_ID, k, v) },
    update: async (write) => {
      const res = await client.saveMany({ updates: [write] });
      const errors = (res.errors ?? []) as Array<{ message?: unknown }>;
      if (errors.length > 0) throw new Error(errors.map((e) => String(e.message ?? '')).join('; '));
    },
    assign: (activityId, categoryId) => client.assignActivityCategory(activityId, SPENDING_TAXONOMY_ID, categoryId),
    record: (draft) => recordCompanionChanges(client, [draft]),
    log,
  };
}
```

---

### Task 9: "Make every <pattern> deposit a payback" (W10)

After a payback filed in full, the Filed screen offers **Make every <pattern> deposit a payback** where "Make this a rule" would be — the pattern from the bank's own words (`bankWords`: before a Venmo note or an Amazon label), trimmed as a category rule's is. The preview says what both rules do and how many deposits waiting for a category they catch now, counted with the sync's own predicate (`ruleApplies`). `makePaybackRules` gates on the installed add-on, then appends the add-on's transaction rule (`paybackMappingRule`: `CREDIT`, `REIMBURSEMENT`, `onlyMoneyIn`, `accountId`) to a list re-read right before it is written, then asks Wealthfolio for a category rule for that account and for `CREDIT` only; a rule already there is not made twice; if Wealthfolio refuses, the transaction rule comes back out (or, when that fails, it is logged so Undo can). Both are change-logged; their Undo and Redo go through the existing kinds, which now carry the limits (`sameMappingRule`, `mappingRuleOf`, a category rule's `activityType`). The mapper honours the limits, and the sync passes it the account.

**Files:**
- Create: `shared/categorize-menu-payback-rules.test.ts`, `shared/payback-rules.test.ts`
- Modify: `companion/src/categorize-payback.test.ts`, `companion/src/categorize.ts`, `companion/src/index.test.ts`, `companion/src/index.ts`, `companion/src/payback-host.test.ts`, `companion/src/payback-host.ts`, `companion/src/wealthfolio.test.ts`, `companion/src/wealthfolio.ts`, `shared/categorize-menu.ts`, `shared/change-entries.ts`, `shared/change-undo-run.ts`, `shared/change-undo.ts`, `shared/mapper.ts`, `shared/payback.ts`, `shared/rule-pattern.ts`, `shared/sync-core.ts`, `shared/types.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 (the copy); Task 3 (`addonPaybackGate`); Task 6 (`paybackResult.offerRules`); Task 8 (`companion/src/payback-host.ts`, the controller and the deps).
- Produces:

```ts
// shared/types.ts — MappingRule gains onlyMoneyIn?: true; accountId?: string
// shared/mapper.ts
export function ruleApplies(rule: MappingRule, description: string, amount: number, wfAccountId?: string): boolean;
export function sameMappingRule(a: MappingRule, b: MappingRule): boolean;
export function paybackMappingRule(pattern: string, wfAccountId: string): MappingRule;
export function mapTransactionWithSource(description, amount, rules, accountType?, wfAccountId?): MappedType;
// shared/rule-pattern.ts
export function bankWords(description: string): string;
export const paybackRulePattern: (description: string) => string;
// shared/payback.ts
export interface PaybackRulesInput { pattern: string; account: { id: string; name: string }; categoryId: string; categoryName: string; description: string }
export interface PaybackRulesIo { gate; secrets; categoryRules(); createCategoryRule(rule); record; log }
export interface PaybackRulesOutcome { ok: boolean; message: string }
export async function makePaybackRules(input: PaybackRulesInput, io: PaybackRulesIo): Promise<PaybackRulesOutcome>;
// shared/categorize-menu.ts — MenuScreen: | { kind: 'paybackRulePreview'; activityId; categoryId } | { kind: 'paybackRulesResult'; activityId; text }
//   MenuAction: | { kind: 'paybackRules'; activityId; categoryId };  export function paybackRuleCatches(session, txn, pattern): number
// shared/change-entries.ts — CategoryRuleFacts gains activityType?: string; MappingRuleFacts.rule gains the limits
// shared/change-undo.ts / change-undo-run.ts — create-rule steps and UndoIo.rules.create carry activityType?
// companion/src/wealthfolio.ts — createCategorizationRule({ …, activityType? })
// companion/src/payback-host.ts — export function companionPaybackRulesIo(client: PaybackRulesClient, log): PaybackRulesIo
// companion/src/categorize.ts — CategorizeDeps gains paybackRules?: (input: PaybackRulesInput) => Promise<PaybackRulesOutcome>
```

```ts
describe('/categorize: "Make every … deposit a payback" (W10)', () => {
  const DONE = 'Done — every VENMO CASHOUT deposit into PNC Checking is a payback under Restaurants from now on. Undo either rule from /changes.';

  it('offers it after the payback, previews it, and makes both rules for the row’s account and words', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => FILED, async () => ({ ok: true, message: DONE }));
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Food & Dining');
    await h.press('Restaurants');
    await h.press('Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback');
    expect(h.last()[0].split('\n')[0]).toBe('Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback?');
    await h.press('Create both rules');
    expect(h.deps.paybackRules).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', account: { id: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' }, categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
      description: 'VENMO CASHOUT',
    });
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(DONE);
    expect(h.labels(h.last()[1])).toEqual(['‹ Back to list', 'Done']);
  });

  it('says why when nothing was made, with nothing to tap twice', async () => {
    const h = setup(async () => FILED, async () => ({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST }));
    await h.toPaybackGrid();
    await h.press('Entertainment');
    await h.press('Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback');
    await h.press('Create both rules');
    expect(h.last()[0]).toBe(PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST);
    expect(h.labels(h.last()[1])).not.toContain('Create both rules');
    expect(h.deps.republish).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```
