**Create `shared/categorize-menu-payback-rules.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { renderScreen, type CategorizeTxn, type MenuAction, type MenuScreen, type MenuSession, type SpendingCategory } from './categorize-menu.js';

const FOOD: SpendingCategory = { id: 'cat-food', name: 'Food & Dining', parentId: null, parentName: null };
const REST: SpendingCategory = { id: 'cat-rest', name: 'Restaurants', parentId: 'cat-food', parentName: 'Food & Dining' };
const deposit = (activityId: string, description: string, over: Partial<CategorizeTxn> = {}): CategorizeTxn => ({
  activityId, date: '2026-10-09', amountCents: 1400, description, accountName: 'PNC_Checking', accountId: 'wf-chk',
  direction: 'in', incomeEligible: true, ...over,
});
const FILED = 'Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes $14 off your Food & Dining spending.\nUndo it from /changes.';
const session = (screen: MenuScreen, txns: CategorizeTxn[] = [deposit('act-7', 'VENMO CASHOUT · Venmo: Dinner')]): MenuSession => ({
  txns, categories: [FOOD, REST], screen, buttons: [], generation: 7, mode: 'categorize',
});
const view = (s: MenuSession) => {
  const r = renderScreen(s);
  const labels = r.keyboard.inline_keyboard.flat().map((b) => b.text);
  return { ...r, labels, action: (label: string): MenuAction => r.buttons[labels.indexOf(label)] };
};
const result = (offerRules: boolean): MenuScreen => ({ kind: 'paybackResult', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest', text: FILED, offerRules });

describe('"Make every … deposit a payback" (W10)', () => {
  it('is offered after a payback filed in full, named by the bank’s words', () => {
    const v = view(session(result(true)));
    expect(v.labels).toEqual(['Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback', 'Next transaction', 'Done']);
    expect(v.action('Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback'))
      .toEqual({ kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'paybackRulePreview', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' } });
  });

  it('is not offered after a partial payback, nor for a row without an account', () => {
    expect(view(session(result(false))).labels).toEqual(['Next transaction', 'Done']);
    expect(view(session(result(true), [deposit('act-7', 'VENMO CASHOUT', { accountId: undefined })])).labels).toEqual(['Next transaction', 'Done']);
  });

  it('previews both rules, escaped, with how many waiting deposits they catch now', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'paybackRulePreview', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' }, [
      deposit('act-7', 'VENMO CASHOUT · Venmo: Dinner'),
      deposit('a', 'VENMO CASHOUT 7781'),
      deposit('out', 'VENMO CASHOUT', { direction: 'out' }),
    ]));
    expect(v.text).toBe([
      'Make every VENMO CASHOUT deposit a payback?',
      'Money into PNC\\_Checking whose description contains "VENMO CASHOUT" becomes a payback under Restaurants (Food & Dining): '
        + 'the sync marks it when it imports, and Wealthfolio files it. Undo either rule from /changes.',
      'Right now that also catches 1 other deposit waiting for a category.',
    ].join('\n'));
    expect(v.action('Create both rules')).toEqual({ kind: 'paybackRules', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' });
    expect(v.labels).toEqual(['Create both rules', '‹ Back to list']);
  });

  it('says when nothing else matches', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'paybackRulePreview', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' }));
    expect(v.text.split('\n')[2]).toBe('Nothing else waiting for a category matches right now.');
  });

  it('shows what making them did, with no way to make them twice', () => {
    const v = view(session({ kind: 'paybackRulesResult', activityId: 'act-7', text: 'Both rules are already there — nothing was added.' }));
    expect(v.text).toBe('Both rules are already there — nothing was added.');
    expect(v.labels).toEqual(['‹ Back to list', 'Done']);
  });

  it('keeps every button within 64 bytes, at any generation', () => {
    for (const screen of [
      result(true),
      { kind: 'paybackRulePreview', activityId: 'act-7', categoryId: 'cat-rest' },
      { kind: 'paybackRulesResult', activityId: 'act-7', text: 'x' },
    ] as MenuScreen[]) {
      const s = { ...session(screen), generation: Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER };
      for (const b of renderScreen(s).keyboard.inline_keyboard.flat()) {
        expect(new TextEncoder().encode(b.callback_data).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(64);
      }
    }
  });
});
```

**Create `shared/payback-rules.test.ts`:**

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, vi } from 'vitest';
import { categoryRuleCreatedChange, mappingRuleCreatedChange, stampChange, type ChangeDraft, type ChangeEntry } from './change-entries.js';
import type { ChangeLogItem } from './change-log.js';
import { MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY, runChangeUndo, UNDO_MAPPING_RULES_UNREADABLE, type UndoIo } from './change-undo-run.js';
import { paybackRuleCatches, type CategorizeTxn } from './categorize-menu.js';
import { createFakeHost } from './fake-host.js';
import { mapTransactionWithSource, paybackMappingRule, ruleApplies, sameMappingRule } from './mapper.js';
import { makePaybackRules, type PaybackRulesInput, type PaybackRulesIo } from './payback.js';
import {
  PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST, PAYBACK_RULES_REFUSED, PAYBACK_RULES_SAVE_FAILED, PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE, PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON, paybackRulesStuck,
} from './payback-copy.js';
import { bankWords, paybackRulePattern } from './rule-pattern.js';
import type { MappingRule } from './types.js';

const NOW = new Date('2026-10-14T15:00:00Z');
const RULE = paybackMappingRule('VENMO CASHOUT', 'wf-chk');
const GLOBAL: MappingRule = { pattern: 'VENMO', matchType: 'contains', activityType: 'TRANSFER_IN' };
const INPUT: PaybackRulesInput = {
  pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', account: { id: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' }, categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
  description: 'VENMO CASHOUT · Venmo: Dinner',
};

describe('a payback rule (W10)', () => {
  it('reads money in only, in its one account', () => {
    expect(RULE).toEqual({
      pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', matchType: 'contains', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT', onlyMoneyIn: true, accountId: 'wf-chk',
    });
    expect(ruleApplies(RULE, 'VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 25, 'wf-chk')).toBe(true);
    expect(ruleApplies(RULE, 'VENMO CASHOUT 1023', -25, 'wf-chk')).toBe(false);
    expect(ruleApplies(RULE, 'VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 0, 'wf-chk')).toBe(false);
    expect(ruleApplies(RULE, 'VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 25, 'wf-sav')).toBe(false);
    expect(ruleApplies(RULE, 'VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 25)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('types money in a payback, and leaves a payment out and another account as they were', () => {
    expect(mapTransactionWithSource('VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 25, [RULE], 'CASH', 'wf-chk')).toEqual({ type: 'CREDIT', fromRule: true, subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' });
    expect(mapTransactionWithSource('VENMO CASHOUT 1023', -25, [RULE], 'CASH', 'wf-chk')).toEqual({ type: 'WITHDRAWAL', fromRule: false });
    expect(mapTransactionWithSource('VENMO CASHOUT 1023', 25, [RULE], 'CASH', 'wf-sav')).toEqual({ type: 'DEPOSIT', fromRule: false });
  });

  it('leaves every older rule as it always read: either way, any account', () => {
    expect(ruleApplies(GLOBAL, 'VENMO PAYMENT', -25)).toBe(true);
    expect(ruleApplies(GLOBAL, 'VENMO PAYMENT', 25, 'wf-any')).toBe(true);
    expect(ruleApplies({ pattern: '(', matchType: 'regex', activityType: 'DEPOSIT' }, 'x', 1)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is a different rule from one with the same words but no limits', () => {
    const plain: MappingRule = { pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', matchType: 'contains', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' };
    expect(sameMappingRule(RULE, { ...RULE })).toBe(true);
    expect(sameMappingRule(RULE, plain)).toBe(false);
    expect(sameMappingRule(RULE, { ...RULE, accountId: 'wf-sav' })).toBe(false);
  });

  it('is built from the bank’s words, not the note the sync added', () => {
    expect(bankWords('VENMO CASHOUT · Venmo: Dinner')).toBe('VENMO CASHOUT');
    expect(bankWords('AMAZON MKTPL*2K4 · Amazon: Lawn')).toBe('AMAZON MKTPL*2K4');
    expect(paybackRulePattern('ZELLE FROM JANE DOE 8ZK1 · Venmo: Rent')).toBe('ZELLE FROM JANE DOE');
  });
});

describe('the preview count', () => {
  const txn = (activityId: string, description: string, over: Partial<CategorizeTxn> = {}): CategorizeTxn => ({
    activityId, date: '2026-10-09', amountCents: 1400, description, accountName: 'PNC Checking', accountId: 'wf-chk', direction: 'in', incomeEligible: true, ...over,
  });
  const rows = [
    txn('self', 'VENMO CASHOUT'),
    txn('a', 'VENMO CASHOUT 7781'),
    txn('b', 'VENMO CASHOUT · Venmo: Rent'),
    txn('out', 'VENMO CASHOUT REVERSAL', { direction: 'out' }),
    txn('other', 'VENMO CASHOUT', { accountId: 'wf-sav' }),
    txn('no', 'PAYROLL'),
  ];

  it('counts exactly the waiting deposits the transaction rule types a payback', () => {
    const signed = (t: CategorizeTxn) => (t.direction === 'out' ? -1 : 1) * t.amountCents / 100;
    const byRule = rows.filter((t) => t.activityId !== 'self'
      && mapTransactionWithSource(bankWords(t.description), signed(t), [RULE], 'CASH', t.accountId).subtype === 'REIMBURSEMENT');
    expect(byRule.map((t) => t.activityId)).toEqual(['a', 'b']);
    expect(paybackRuleCatches({ txns: rows }, rows[0], 'VENMO CASHOUT')).toBe(byRule.length);
  });
});

/** The add-on's secrets, Wealthfolio's rules, and the change log, in memory. */
function world(over: { mapping?: string | null; wfRules?: Array<Record<string, any>>; io?: Partial<PaybackRulesIo> } = {}) {
  const data = new Map<string, string>();
  if (over.mapping !== null) data.set(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY, over.mapping ?? JSON.stringify([GLOBAL]));
  const wfRules: Array<Record<string, any>> = [...(over.wfRules ?? [])];
  const logged: ChangeEntry[] = [];
  const io: PaybackRulesIo = {
    gate: async () => null,
    secrets: { get: async (k) => data.get(k) ?? null, set: vi.fn(async (k: string, v: string) => { data.set(k, v); }) },
    categoryRules: async () => wfRules.map((r) => ({ ...r })) as any,
    createCategoryRule: vi.fn(async (r) => { wfRules.push({ id: 'rule-9', ...r }); return 'rule-9'; }),
    record: vi.fn(async (d: ChangeDraft) => { logged.push(stampChange(d, 'companion', NOW)); }),
    log: vi.fn(),
    ...(over.io ?? {}),
  };
  const mapping = () => JSON.parse(data.get(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY) ?? '[]') as MappingRule[];
  return { io, data, wfRules, logged, mapping };
}

describe('makePaybackRules', () => {
  it('appends the transaction rule, makes Wealthfolio’s rule for paybacks in this account, and logs both', async () => {
    const w = world();
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({
      ok: true, message: 'Done — every VENMO CASHOUT deposit into PNC Checking is a payback under Restaurants from now on. Undo either rule from /changes.',
    });
    expect(w.mapping()).toEqual([GLOBAL, RULE]);
    expect(w.io.createCategoryRule).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
      name: 'Telegram payback: VENMO CASHOUT', pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT',
    });
    expect(w.logged.map((e) => [e.kind, e.summary])).toEqual([
      ['mapping-rule-created', 'Added a transaction rule: "VENMO CASHOUT" deposits into PNC Checking are paybacks'],
      ['category-rule-created', 'Created a rule: paybacks containing "VENMO CASHOUT" in PNC Checking go to Restaurants'],
    ]);
    expect(w.logged[1].after).toMatchObject({ ruleId: 'rule-9', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT' });
  });

  it('waits for an add-on that reads the rule’s limits, and writes nothing', async () => {
    const w = world({ io: { gate: async () => PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON } });
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON });
    expect(w.io.secrets.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(w.io.createCategoryRule).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('makes nothing twice', async () => {
    const both = world({
      mapping: JSON.stringify([RULE]),
      wfRules: [{ id: 'r1', pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT' }],
    });
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, both.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST });
    expect(both.io.secrets.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const one = world({ mapping: JSON.stringify([RULE]) });
    expect((await makePaybackRules(INPUT, one.io)).ok).toBe(true);
    expect(one.mapping()).toEqual([RULE]);
    expect(one.logged.map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(['category-rule-created']);
  });

  it('never rewrites a transaction-rule list it cannot read', async () => {
    const w = world({ mapping: '{"not":"a list"}' });
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: UNDO_MAPPING_RULES_UNREADABLE });
    expect(w.io.secrets.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(w.io.createCategoryRule).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('says it couldn’t reach Wealthfolio when its rules can’t be read, and writes nothing', async () => {
    const w = world({ io: { categoryRules: async () => { throw new Error('ECONNREFUSED'); } } });
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE });
    expect(w.io.secrets.set).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('stops when the transaction rule can’t be saved', async () => {
    const w = world();
    w.io.secrets.set = async () => { throw new Error('500'); };
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_RULES_SAVE_FAILED });
    expect(w.io.createCategoryRule).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it('takes the transaction rule back out when Wealthfolio refuses the category rule, so neither is half made', async () => {
    const w = world({ io: { createCategoryRule: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('400 - accountId is required'); }) } });
    const out = await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io);
    expect(out).toEqual({ ok: false, message: PAYBACK_RULES_REFUSED });
    expect(out.message).not.toContain('accountId');
    expect(w.mapping()).toEqual([GLOBAL]);
    expect(w.logged).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('says so, and logs the transaction rule for Undo, when it cannot take it back out', async () => {
    const w = world({ io: { createCategoryRule: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error('400'); }) } });
    let writes = 0;
    const set = w.io.secrets.set;
    w.io.secrets.set = async (k, v) => { writes += 1; if (writes > 1) throw new Error('500'); await set(k, v); };
    expect(await makePaybackRules(INPUT, w.io)).toEqual({ ok: false, message: paybackRulesStuck('VENMO CASHOUT') });
    expect(w.mapping()).toEqual([GLOBAL, RULE]);
    expect(w.logged.map((e) => e.kind)).toEqual(['mapping-rule-created']);
  });
});

describe('Undo and Redo of the two rules', () => {
  const NAME = 'Telegram payback: VENMO CASHOUT';
  /** The runner over the add-on's secrets and Wealthfolio's rules. */
  function undoWorld(mapping: MappingRule[], wfRules: Array<{ id: string; name: string; pattern: string }>) {
    const data = new Map([[MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY, JSON.stringify(mapping)]]);
    const log: ChangeEntry[] = [];
    const create = vi.fn(async (_r: Record<string, unknown>) => 'rule-10');
    const io: UndoIo = {
      host: createFakeHost({}).host,
      secrets: { get: async (k) => data.get(k) ?? null, set: async (k, v) => { data.set(k, v); } },
      rules: {
        list: async () => wfRules,
        create,
        remove: vi.fn(async (id: string) => { wfRules.splice(wfRules.findIndex((r) => r.id === id), 1); }),
        filedBy: async () => [],
      },
      record: async (d) => { log.unshift(stampChange(d, 'companion', NOW)); },
      where: { host: 'companion', companionVersion: '1.62.0' },
    };
    const item = (e: ChangeEntry): ChangeLogItem => {
      const latest = log.find((x) => x.reverses?.id === e.id);
      return latest?.reverses?.op === 'undo' ? { ...e, undone: { by: latest.id, at: latest.at } } : { ...e };
    };
    const run = (e: ChangeEntry, op: 'undo' | 'redo') => runChangeUndo(item(e), [...log], op, io, 'plain', NOW);
    return { data, io, run, create, wfRules, mapping: () => JSON.parse(data.get(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY)!) as MappingRule[] };
  }

  it('Undo of the transaction rule takes out exactly it; Redo puts it back with its limits', async () => {
    const lookalike: MappingRule = { pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', matchType: 'contains', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: 'REIMBURSEMENT' };
    const w = undoWorld([GLOBAL, lookalike, RULE], []);
    const entry = stampChange(mappingRuleCreatedChange({ rule: RULE, description: 'VENMO CASHOUT', account: { wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' } }), 'companion', NOW);
    expect(await w.run(entry, 'undo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
    expect(w.mapping()).toEqual([GLOBAL, lookalike]);
    expect(await w.run(entry, 'redo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true });
    expect(w.mapping()).toEqual([GLOBAL, lookalike, RULE]);
  });

  it('Undo of Wealthfolio’s rule deletes it; Redo makes it again for paybacks in that account', async () => {
    const w = undoWorld([], [{ id: 'rule-9', name: NAME, pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT' }]);
    const entry = stampChange(categoryRuleCreatedChange({
      ruleId: 'rule-9', name: NAME, pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', categoryName: 'Restaurants',
      account: { wfId: 'wf-chk', name: 'PNC Checking' }, activityType: 'CREDIT',
    }), 'companion', NOW);
    expect(await w.run(entry, 'undo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true, message: 'Undone — the rule is gone.' });
    expect(w.wfRules).toEqual([]);
    expect(await w.run(entry, 'redo')).toMatchObject({ ok: true, message: 'Redone — the rule is back.' });
    expect(w.create).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ name: NAME, pattern: 'VENMO CASHOUT', categoryId: 'cat-rest', accountId: 'wf-chk', activityType: 'CREDIT' });
  });
});
```

```ts
}

/** What "Make every … deposit a payback" needs of a `WealthfolioClient`. */
export type PaybackRulesClient = SecretClient & Pick<WealthfolioClient, 'listCategorizationRules' | 'createCategorizationRule'>;

/** W10 on the companion: the add-on's transaction rules (a secret) and
 *  Wealthfolio's categorization rules, at the same LOSING priority (50) and in
 *  the spending taxonomy as /categorize's rules. */
export function companionPaybackRulesIo(client: PaybackRulesClient, log: (msg: string) => void): PaybackRulesIo {
  const get = (k: string) => client.getAddonSecret(ADDON_ID, k);
  return {
    gate: () => addonPaybackGate(get),
    secrets: { get, set: (k, v) => client.setAddonSecret(ADDON_ID, k, v) },
    categoryRules: async () => (await client.listCategorizationRules()).map((r) => ({
      id: r.id, pattern: r.pattern, categoryId: r.categoryId,
      ...(r.accountId ? { accountId: r.accountId } : {}), ...(r.activityType ? { activityType: r.activityType } : {}),
    })),
    createCategoryRule: async (rule) => (await client.createCategorizationRule({ ...rule, taxonomyId: SPENDING_TAXONOMY_ID, priority: 50 })) ?? null,
    record: (draft) => recordCompanionChanges(client, [draft]),
    log,
  };
}
```

with:

```ts
}

/** A payback's outcome: the runner's words, escaped — a bank or category name
 *  can carry `_` or `*` — and the way on. A payback filed in full offers to
 *  make every deposit like it one (W10) where "Make this a rule" would be: the
 *  rules need the row's account and a pattern from its bank words. */
function renderPaybackResult(session: MenuSession, screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'paybackResult' }>): RenderResult {
  const txn = findTxn(session, screen.activityId);
  const pattern = txn ? paybackRulePattern(txn.description) : '';
  const offer: Btn[][] = screen.offerRules && txn?.accountId && pattern
    ? [[{
      text: paybackRulesButton(pattern),
      action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'paybackRulePreview', activityId: screen.activityId, categoryId: screen.categoryId } },
    }]]
    : [];
  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    ...offer,
    [{ text: 'Next transaction', action: { kind: 'goto', screen: { kind: 'list', page: 0 } } }],
    [DONE_BTN],
  ];
  return finish(session.generation, escapeMarkdown(screen.text), rows);
}

/** Other deposits waiting for a category that the transaction rule would make
 *  paybacks now: money in, in the same account, matched on their bank words by
 *  the sync's own predicate — so the count is the rule's, not a guess. */
export function paybackRuleCatches(session: Pick<MenuSession, 'txns'>, txn: CategorizeTxn, pattern: string): number {
  if (!txn.accountId) return 0;
  const rule = paybackMappingRule(pattern, txn.accountId);
  return session.txns.filter((t) => t.activityId !== txn.activityId && t.direction === 'in'
    && ruleApplies(rule, bankWords(t.description), Math.abs(t.amountCents) / 100, t.accountId)).length;
}

function renderPaybackRulePreview(session: MenuSession, screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'paybackRulePreview' }>): RenderResult {
  const txn = findTxn(session, screen.activityId);
  const category = findCategory(session, screen.categoryId);
  if (!txn || !category || !txn.accountId) return renderList(session, 0, GONE_NOTE);
  const pattern = paybackRulePattern(txn.description);
  const text = escapeMarkdown(paybackRulesPreview(pattern, txn.accountName, categoryDisplayName(category), paybackRuleCatches(session, txn, pattern)));
  const rows: Btn[][] = [
    [{ text: PAYBACK_RULES_CREATE, action: { kind: 'paybackRules', activityId: screen.activityId, categoryId: screen.categoryId } }],
    [BACK_TO_LIST_BTN],
  ];
  return finish(session.generation, text, rows);
}

/** What making the rules did, escaped. Nothing here makes them again. */
function renderPaybackRulesResult(session: MenuSession, screen: Extract<MenuScreen, { kind: 'paybackRulesResult' }>): RenderResult {
  return finish(session.generation, escapeMarkdown(screen.text), [[BACK_TO_LIST_BTN], [DONE_BTN]]);
}
```

```ts
  categoryName: string;

/** A Wealthfolio categorization rule created from Telegram. Undo (R2): delete it.
 *  A scoped one names its account, and its Redo re-creates it scoped. */
export function categoryRuleCreatedChange(f: CategoryRuleFacts): ChangeDraft {
  const where = f.account ? ` in ${f.account.name}` : '';
  return {
    actor: 'you',
    kind: 'category-rule-created',
    ...(f.account ? { account: { wfId: f.account.wfId, name: f.account.name } } : {}),
    subject: { ...(f.ruleId ? { ruleId: f.ruleId } : {}), rulePattern: f.pattern, categoryName: f.categoryName },
    summary: `Created a rule: descriptions containing "${f.pattern}"${where} go to ${f.categoryName}`,
    after: {
      ruleId: f.ruleId, name: f.name, pattern: f.pattern, categoryId: f.categoryId, categoryName: f.categoryName,
      ...(f.account ? { accountId: f.account.wfId } : {}),
    },
    undoable: true,
  };
}

export interface MappingRuleFacts {
  rule: { pattern: string; matchType: string; activityType: string; subtype?: string };
  /** The transaction it was taught from. */
  description?: string;
  account?: ChangeAccount;
}

/** A transaction rule added to `mapping_rules` (the transfer-learning menu). */
export function mappingRuleCreatedChange(f: MappingRuleFacts): ChangeDraft {
  const direction = f.rule.activityType === 'TRANSFER_IN' ? 'a transfer in'
    : f.rule.activityType === 'TRANSFER_OUT' ? 'a transfer out'
      : f.rule.activityType;
  return {
    actor: 'you',
    kind: 'mapping-rule-created',
    ...(f.account ? { account: f.account } : {}),
    subject: { rulePattern: f.rule.pattern, ...(f.description ? { description: f.description } : {}) },
    summary: `Added a transaction rule: "${f.rule.pattern}" is ${direction}`,
    after: { rule: { ...f.rule } },
    undoable: true,
  };
```

with:

```ts
  categoryName: string;
  /** The one account the rule is scoped to (3, L5); absent, every account. */
  account?: { wfId: string; name: string };
  /** The one activity type it files (W10: `CREDIT`, paybacks only); absent, every type. */
  activityType?: string;
}

/** A Wealthfolio categorization rule created from Telegram. Undo (R2): delete it.
 *  A scoped one names its account, and its Redo re-creates it scoped. */
export function categoryRuleCreatedChange(f: CategoryRuleFacts): ChangeDraft {
  const where = f.account ? ` in ${f.account.name}` : '';
  const which = f.activityType === 'CREDIT' ? 'paybacks' : 'descriptions';
  return {
    actor: 'you',
    kind: 'category-rule-created',
    ...(f.account ? { account: { wfId: f.account.wfId, name: f.account.name } } : {}),
    subject: { ...(f.ruleId ? { ruleId: f.ruleId } : {}), rulePattern: f.pattern, categoryName: f.categoryName },
    summary: `Created a rule: ${which} containing "${f.pattern}"${where} go to ${f.categoryName}`,
    after: {
      ruleId: f.ruleId, name: f.name, pattern: f.pattern, categoryId: f.categoryId, categoryName: f.categoryName,
      ...(f.account ? { accountId: f.account.wfId } : {}),
      ...(f.activityType ? { activityType: f.activityType } : {}),
    },
    undoable: true,
  };
}

export interface MappingRuleFacts {
  rule: { pattern: string; matchType: string; activityType: string; subtype?: string; onlyMoneyIn?: true; accountId?: string };
  /** The transaction it was taught from. */
  description?: string;
  account?: ChangeAccount;
}

/** A transaction rule added to `mapping_rules` (the transfer-learning menu, or
 *  "Make every … deposit a payback", W10). */
export function mappingRuleCreatedChange(f: MappingRuleFacts): ChangeDraft {
  const direction = f.rule.activityType === 'TRANSFER_IN' ? 'a transfer in'
    : f.rule.activityType === 'TRANSFER_OUT' ? 'a transfer out'
      : f.rule.activityType;
  const payback = f.rule.activityType === 'CREDIT' && f.rule.subtype === 'REIMBURSEMENT' && f.rule.onlyMoneyIn === true;
  return {
    actor: 'you',
    kind: 'mapping-rule-created',
    ...(f.account ? { account: f.account } : {}),
    subject: { rulePattern: f.rule.pattern, ...(f.description ? { description: f.description } : {}) },
    summary: payback
      ? `Added a transaction rule: "${f.rule.pattern}" deposits${f.account ? ` into ${f.account.name}` : ''} are paybacks`
      : `Added a transaction rule: "${f.rule.pattern}" is ${direction}`,
    after: { rule: { ...f.rule } },
    undoable: true,
  };
```

```ts
function matchRule(description: string, rules: MappingRule[]): MappingRule | null {
  for (const rule of rules) {
    if (rule.matchType === 'contains') {
      if (description.toLowerCase().includes(rule.pattern.toLowerCase())) {
        return rule;
      }
    } else {
      // Skip rules with invalid regex rather than crashing a whole sync run.
      // RuleEditor surfaces the error at save/preview time so the user can fix it.
      let re: RegExp;
      try {
        re = new RegExp(rule.pattern, 'i');
      } catch {
        continue;
      }
      if (re.test(description)) {
        return rule;
      }
    }
  }
  return null;
}

// `capital one transfer` earned its place the hard way (2026-09-12): two real
```

with:

```ts
  subtype?: string;
}

/**
 * Whether one transaction rule decides this transaction. A payback rule
 * ("Make every … deposit a payback", 1.62 W10) carries two limits no older
 * rule has: `onlyMoneyIn` — it reads money in only, so a payment out that
 * shares the pattern is never typed a CREDIT (a rule's type is otherwise
 * direction-blind) — and `accountId`, the one Wealthfolio account it reads.
 */
export function ruleApplies(rule: MappingRule, description: string, amount: number, wfAccountId?: string): boolean {
  if (rule.onlyMoneyIn && !(amount > 0)) return false;
  if (rule.accountId && rule.accountId !== wfAccountId) return false;
  if (rule.matchType === 'contains') return description.toLowerCase().includes(rule.pattern.toLowerCase());
  // Skip rules with invalid regex rather than crashing a whole sync run.
  // RuleEditor surfaces the error at save/preview time so the user can fix it.
  try {
    return new RegExp(rule.pattern, 'i').test(description);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function matchRule(description: string, rules: MappingRule[], amount: number, wfAccountId?: string): MappingRule | null {
  return rules.find((rule) => ruleApplies(rule, description, amount, wfAccountId)) ?? null;
}

/** The same transaction rule: pattern, match, type, subtype and the payback limits. */
export function sameMappingRule(a: MappingRule, b: MappingRule): boolean {
  return a.pattern === b.pattern && a.matchType === b.matchType && a.activityType === b.activityType
    && (a.subtype ?? '') === (b.subtype ?? '') && !!a.onlyMoneyIn === !!b.onlyMoneyIn && (a.accountId ?? '') === (b.accountId ?? '');
}

/** The transaction rule W10 adds: money into one account whose bank text
 *  contains the pattern is a CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT — a payback. */
export function paybackMappingRule(pattern: string, wfAccountId: string): MappingRule {
  return { pattern, matchType: 'contains', activityType: 'CREDIT', subtype: REIMBURSEMENT_SUBTYPE, onlyMoneyIn: true, accountId: wfAccountId };
}
```

```ts
// ── "Make every … deposit a payback" (W10) ───────────────────────────────

/** What the rules are called in Wealthfolio's rules list (the pattern is never cut). */
export const PAYBACK_RULE_NAME_PREFIX = 'Telegram payback: ';
const RULE_NAME_MAX = 60;

/** The offer, as the Filed screen makes it. */
export interface PaybackRulesInput {
  /** `paybackRulePattern` of the deposit's description. */
  pattern: string;
  /** The deposit's Wealthfolio account: both rules read only it. */
  account: { id: string; name: string };
  categoryId: string;
  categoryName: string;
  /** The deposit it was made from, for the change log. */
  description: string;
}

/** What a host lends `makePaybackRules`. */
export interface PaybackRulesIo {
  /** As `PaybackIo.gate`: an older add-on would read the transaction rule without its limits. */
  gate(): Promise<string | null>;
  secrets: { get: SecretGet; set: SecretSet };
  /** Wealthfolio's categorization rules now. Rejects when unreachable. */
  categoryRules(): Promise<Array<{ id: string; pattern: string; categoryId: string | null; accountId?: string; activityType?: string }>>;
  /** Creates a contains rule for one account and one activity type; resolves its id (null when the reply named none). */
  createCategoryRule(rule: { name: string; pattern: string; categoryId: string; accountId: string; activityType: string }): Promise<string | null>;
  record(draft: ChangeDraft): Promise<void>;
  log(msg: string): void;
}

export interface PaybackRulesOutcome {
  ok: boolean;
  message: string;
}

/** `mapping_rules`, read strictly: nothing stored is none; anything but a list is unreadable (null) and never rewritten. */
function parseMappingRules(raw: string | null): MappingRule[] | null {
  if (raw === null || raw.trim() === '') return [];
  try {
    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    return Array.isArray(parsed) ? (parsed as MappingRule[]) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Make every <pattern> deposit into one account a payback under a category
 * (W10): an add-on transaction rule — money in only, this account only, a
 * CREDIT marked REIMBURSEMENT — which the sync applies on import, then
 * Wealthfolio's categorization rule for the category, which files only
 * CREDITs (the paybacks) in this account. A rule that is already there is not
 * made twice. The transaction rule is appended to a list re-read right before
 * the write; if Wealthfolio then refuses the category rule, the transaction
 * rule comes back out, so neither is half made — and when that write fails
 * too, it is logged so Undo can take it out. Both rules are change-logged.
 */
export async function makePaybackRules(input: PaybackRulesInput, io: PaybackRulesIo): Promise<PaybackRulesOutcome> {
  const no = (message: string): PaybackRulesOutcome => ({ ok: false, message });
  const gate = await io.gate().catch(() => PAYBACK_UPDATE_ADDON);
  if (gate) return no(gate);
  const rule = paybackMappingRule(input.pattern, input.account.id);

  let list: MappingRule[] | null;
  let haveCategoryRule: boolean;
  try {
    list = parseMappingRules(await io.secrets.get(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY));
    haveCategoryRule = (await io.categoryRules()).some((r) => r.pattern === input.pattern && r.categoryId === input.categoryId
      && r.accountId === input.account.id && r.activityType === 'CREDIT');
  } catch (err) {
    io.log(`Payback rules for "${input.pattern}": ${errorText(err)}`);
    return no(PAYBACK_UNREACHABLE);
  }
  if (list === null) return no(UNDO_MAPPING_RULES_UNREADABLE);
  const haveMapping = list.some((r) => sameMappingRule(r, rule));
  if (haveMapping && haveCategoryRule) return no(PAYBACK_RULES_EXIST);

  const log = async (draft: ChangeDraft) => {
    try {
      await io.record(draft);
    } catch (err) {
      io.log(`Payback rules for "${input.pattern}": the change log was not written: ${errorText(err)}`);
    }
  };
  const mappingEntry = () => mappingRuleCreatedChange({ rule, description: input.description, account: { wfId: input.account.id, name: input.account.name } });

  // 1. The transaction rule, appended to the list as it is right now.
  let added = false;
  if (!haveMapping) {
    try {
      const fresh = parseMappingRules(await io.secrets.get(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY));
      if (fresh === null) return no(UNDO_MAPPING_RULES_UNREADABLE);
      if (!fresh.some((r) => sameMappingRule(r, rule))) {
        await io.secrets.set(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY, JSON.stringify([...fresh, rule]));
        added = true;
      }
    } catch (err) {
      io.log(`Payback rules for "${input.pattern}": the transaction rule was not saved: ${errorText(err)}`);
      return no(PAYBACK_RULES_SAVE_FAILED);
    }
  }

  // 2. Wealthfolio's category rule: paybacks (CREDIT) in this account only.
  const name = `${PAYBACK_RULE_NAME_PREFIX}${input.pattern}`.slice(0, RULE_NAME_MAX);
  let ruleId: string | null = null;
  if (!haveCategoryRule) {
    try {
      ruleId = await io.createCategoryRule({ name, pattern: input.pattern, categoryId: input.categoryId, accountId: input.account.id, activityType: 'CREDIT' });
    } catch (err) {
      io.log(`Payback rules for "${input.pattern}": Wealthfolio refused the category rule: ${errorText(err)}`);
      if (!added) return no(PAYBACK_RULES_REFUSED);
      try {
        const now = parseMappingRules(await io.secrets.get(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY));
        if (now === null) throw new Error('the transaction rules are unreadable');
        await io.secrets.set(MAPPING_RULES_SECRET_KEY, JSON.stringify(now.filter((r) => !sameMappingRule(r, rule))));
      } catch (again) {
        io.log(`Payback rules for "${input.pattern}": taking the transaction rule back failed: ${errorText(again)}`);
        await log(mappingEntry());
        return no(paybackRulesStuck(input.pattern));
      }
      return no(PAYBACK_RULES_REFUSED);
    }
  }

  // 3. The change log, which never costs the rules.
  if (added) await log(mappingEntry());
  if (!haveCategoryRule) {
    await log(categoryRuleCreatedChange({
      ruleId, name, pattern: input.pattern, categoryId: input.categoryId, categoryName: input.categoryName,
      account: { wfId: input.account.id, name: input.account.name }, activityType: 'CREDIT',
    }));
  }
  return { ok: true, message: paybackRulesDone(input.pattern, input.account.name, input.categoryName) };
}
```

```ts
/** The marks the sync appends to a bank description (a Venmo note, an Amazon label). */
const ENRICHMENT_MARKS = [' · Venmo: ', ' · Amazon: '];

/** A stored description's own bank words: before the Venmo note or the Amazon
 *  label the sync appends. Transaction rules match the bank's text, before
 *  either is added. */
export function bankWords(description: string): string {
  const cut = ENRICHMENT_MARKS.map((m) => description.indexOf(m)).filter((i) => i >= 0);
  return cut.length ? description.slice(0, Math.min(...cut)) : description;
}

/** The pattern "Make every … deposit a payback" (W10) is built from: the
 *  bank's words, trimmed as a category rule's are. */
export const paybackRulePattern = (description: string): string => categoryRulePattern(bankWords(description));
```

Edit 1 of 1 in `CHANGELOG.md` — replace:

```markdown

## [Unreleased]

## [1.61.0] - 2026-10-14

### Added
```

with:

```markdown

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- **Paybacks.** A deposit that pays you back for spending — a friend's Venmo
  for dinner — is filed against that spending. In `/categorize`, a money-in
  row's **It's a payback »** (it read "Spending categories »") shows the
  spending categories; picking one re-types the row to a `CREDIT` marked
  `REIMBURSEMENT` — since Wealthfolio 3.9 the only money in it takes off
  spending — and files it: "Filed as a payback under Restaurants — it takes
  $14 off your Food & Dining spending." A new sync override,
  `asReimbursement`, keeps the sync from turning it back into a deposit while
  the bank row is money in on a cash account. Undo in `/changes` takes the
  override off, makes the row a deposit again and takes the category off;
  Redo does it all again. Refused, each in its own words and with nothing
  written: a transfer, half of a linked transfer, a pending row, a card, a row
  that is not a deposit or is already a payback, one not from the bank feed,
  and an unreadable overrides document. A step that fails partway says what
  stands.
- **Make every <pattern> deposit a payback.** After a payback, the Filed
  screen offers this instead of "Make this a rule": an add-on transaction rule
  — money into that account only, matched on the bank's own words — which the
  sync applies on import, and a Wealthfolio category rule that files those
  paybacks under the category. The preview says how many deposits waiting for
  a category it catches now. Both rules are in the change log, with Undo.

### Changed
- Paybacks need both halves on 1.62.0. Telegram's payback, its Undo and Redo
  and the two-rule offer wait for an installed add-on of 1.62.0 or newer —
  an older one ignores the new override and would turn the row back into a
  deposit — and the app's Change log waits for a companion of 1.62.0 before it
  points a payback's Undo at Telegram.

### Fixed
- `/categorize` and `/recategorize` never show Wealthfolio's own error text
  when it turns a category down: a filing, a move or a move's Undo that
  Wealthfolio refuses says so in the add-on's words, with the reason when
  Wealthfolio gave one. Picking a spending category for a deposit used to end
  there, on Wealthfolio's raw refusal.

## [1.61.0] - 2026-10-14

### Added
```

```markdown
  Commands that need details — `/pool`, `/newrule`, `/afford` — ask for them
  one question at a time; the one-line forms still work. Bare `/pool` shows
  the pool with Set and Clear buttons.
```

```markdown
## 17. Paybacks (2026-10-14, plan `2026-10-14-paybacks.md`)

1.62.0 part 2 — W6–W10 of `2026-10-14-line-up-and-paybacks-design.md`.
Lining up (W1–W5) and the platform work (U1–U4) are planned on their own
branches.

- **W11 (paybacks in the app) is next**, its own small plan. It consumes the
  fixed interface this part ships: the `asReimbursement` override, the
  `payback-filed` change kind (`paybackFiledChange`), `PAYBACK_SINCE` /
  `companionUnderstandsPaybacks`, and the pure `planPayback`.
- **Telegram's paybacks wait for an installed add-on of 1.62.0.** A companion
  that cannot see the add-ons directory reads it as unknown and refuses, as
  the Venmo note's Undo does; W11 then offers paybacks from the app.
- **The override yields** when the bank row turns into money out, or the
  account stops being a cash account: the row is typed as usual, and keeps the
  `REIMBURSEMENT` subtype (Expense either way on a cash `WITHDRAWAL`) and its
  category. Like every transaction override it is pruned a year after it was
  set, on the next write of the document — long after the row left the sync's
  window.
- **A re-type writes back the stored instant exactly** (`activityDate` as
  Wealthfolio reported it), so a payback never moves a row to another day —
  W1's 12:00 UTC rows stay at 12:00.
- **The payback's Undo is in `/changes`.** The Filed screen says so; it has no
  ↩ Undo of its own. The app's Change log says "Undo this from Telegram:
  /changes" (the add-on SDK cannot take a category off).
- **The one-tap guess is not offered on the payback grid**: every tap there
  goes through the payback.
- **The transaction rule's limits are not shown** in Settings → Transaction
  rules (money in only, one account); editing the rule there keeps them, and
  the "Test a description" box never matches a rule scoped to an account. A
  reader before 1.62 ignores the limits — the reason the offer waits for an
  installed add-on of 1.62.0.
- **The transaction rule retypes matching money in already imported** inside
  the sync's window, including a deposit filed under an income category; that
  income filing stays beside the spending one Wealthfolio's rule adds (its
  rule run fills only an empty spending slot). The preview counts only
  deposits waiting for a category.
- **A stuck payback** — the override saved, Wealthfolio refusing the re-type,
  and the override not taken back — is said so: the next sync makes the row a
  payback with no category, which `/categorize` then offers.
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run every new test file with the date forced to 2027-01-01**

Nothing may depend on the real clock. A throwaway setup file fakes `Date` before every test; delete it afterwards — none of it is committed.

```sh
cat > zz-2027.setup.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, vi } from 'vitest';
beforeEach(() => { vi.useFakeTimers({ toFake: ['Date'] }); vi.setSystemTime(new Date('2027-01-01T12:00:00Z')); });
EOF
cp zz-2027.setup.ts companion/zz-2027.setup.ts
cat > zz-2027.config.mts <<'EOF'
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
export default defineConfig({ test: { environment: 'jsdom', globals: true, setupFiles: ['./src/test-setup.ts', './zz-2027.setup.ts'] } });
EOF
cat > companion/zz-2027.config.mts <<'EOF'
import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config';
export default defineConfig({ test: { globals: true, setupFiles: ['./zz-2027.setup.ts'] } });
EOF
npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts shared/payback-gate.test.ts shared/payback.test.ts shared/payback-file.test.ts shared/change-undo-payback.test.ts shared/sync-core-payback.test.ts shared/categorize-menu-payback.test.ts shared/assign-refusal.test.ts shared/payback-rules.test.ts shared/categorize-menu-payback-rules.test.ts
(cd companion && npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts src/payback-host.test.ts src/categorize-payback.test.ts src/categorize.test.ts src/wealthfolio.test.ts)
(cd companion && npx vitest run --config zz-2027.config.mts src/index.test.ts -t "a payback")
rm zz-2027.setup.ts zz-2027.config.mts companion/zz-2027.setup.ts companion/zz-2027.config.mts
git status --short
```
